**Key findings:**
- THE LHC THESIS (VERIFIED). LHC = the Logos Hyperlattice Compiler. The programme's cross-cutting empirical claim (PROGRAMME.md §1): the LHC 'realizes this stack as a single propagator network in which compile-time and run-time are the same fixpoint computation, the .pnet serialization is its own IR, and the network is scheduler-portable up to CALM-permitted variation.' This is a SYSTEMS/ARTIFACT claim ('describes what exists; no new theorems' — A0 risk profile), not a theorem. It sits atop a 4-level thesis tree: Substrate (FL(ℵ₀)+Whitman) / Syntax (Poly functors on lattice cells = Sussman-Radul propagators) / Demarcation (CALM) / Recovery (Layered Recovery Principle), plus an optimality axis (per-variety Hasse = optimal parallel decomposition).
- PHASE COLLAPSE — HOW WE FRAME IT (VERIFIED, the core answer to the human's question). 'Multi-pass-compiler-as-single-fixpoint' is A0 headline H2. Precise statement (audit Claim 1): the LHC implements parsing, elaboration, type checking, constraint resolution, retraction, evaluation/reduction, and (via .pnet) cross-session caching as fixpoint computations on ONE propagator network of lattice-valued cells; compile-time and run-time use the SAME primitives (cells, propagators, BSP scheduler, CHAMP persistence); 'the phase boundary between any two of these reduces to a STRATUM BOUNDARY in the LRP sense — a stratification on the same network, not a separation between distinct artifacts.' Conventional phases (elaboration, type-check, optimizer, runtime verification) become strata, not pipeline stages.
- DETERMINISTIC PARALLELISM — HOW WE FRAME IT (VERIFIED). A0 headline H1: the propagator network's fixpoint is INVARIANT under propagator firing order. Architecturally enforced by lattice-valued cells + monotone propagators + semilattice (commutative/associative/idempotent) merge. Framed as 'the LVars / CALM / BloomL lineage realized at the granularity of an ENTIRE programming-language COMPILER, not just a runtime data-structure library.' Empirical anchor: 4 schedulers (Gauss-Seidel sequential DFS; BSP/Jacobi parallel; widening variant; Zig BSP PoC over serialized .pnet) produce the same fixpoint; bench-scheduler-ab.rkt overrides current-use-bsp-scheduler? GLOBALLY (catches unify, elab-speculation, bridges, tabling — not one subsystem).
- THE COUPLING IS THE STORY (VERIFIED — the single most important structural claim). H1 is the CAUSE; H2 is the CONSEQUENCE. The dependency chain: lattice-valued cells + monotone propagators + semilattice merge => order-invariant fixpoint => scheduler-portable network state => .pnet-as-IR => no preferred phase ordering => no phase distinction => phase collapse. CALM (and the LVars deterministic-parallelism lineage) is therefore 'the LOAD-BEARING INVARIANT for the entire LHC architecture.' The audit explicitly states this two-claim coupling is 'a stronger story than either headline alone.'
- E9-bis — THE EMPIRICAL SIGNAL FOR CALM-AS-STRUCTURAL (VERIFIED, owner-attested, high rhetorical value). The PAR Track 2 parallel scheduler was a ~40-line PoC; the expected outcome was 8+ hours debugging race conditions / system-wide test failures; the ACTUAL outcome was it 'just worked' on first run, requiring double/triple-verification because the result was hard to believe. The audit reads this as non-trivial empirical evidence that CALM-correctness is INHERITED FOR FREE by the substrate (structural, not scheduler-implementation property) — a 40-line naive parallel rewrite should not work first-try unless the monotone-fixpoint-over-semilattice invariant is enforced architecturally.
- C4.f — THE CATEGORICAL WITNESS FOR PHASE COLLAPSE (VERIFIED, structural conjecture). The PReduce substrate note's deepest result: phase collapse is operationally witnessed by the SAME Kleene-star/trace operator firing on different CELL CONTENTS — typing cells at 'compile time,' reduction cells at 'run time.' 'The algorithm is invariant; only the cells differ.' Run-to-quiescence on the propagator network IS the GoI machine structurally (geometric series I+σπ+(σπ)²+... = Kleene star (σπ)* in a traced semilattice-enriched SMC; Haghverdi-Scott 2004). This gives A0's H2 a categorical foundation — labeled 'Structural conjecture (load-bearing for Paper A0),' pending implementation discipline of typing-cell + reduction-cell on the same scheduler.
- RIGOR GRADIENT IS EXPLICITLY TRACKED (VERIFIED — crucial honesty signal). The corpus uses a disciplined strength ladder: Theorem (cited+read) / Engineered-and-tested (code+PIRs exist) / Structural conjecture (substrate suggests, unproved) / Asserted / Speculative bridging (drawn from adjacent math, useful-if-holds, NOT externally citable). The LHC SYSTEMS claim (A0) is RIGOROUS: it describes shipped engineering (.pnet cache ON by default, 4 schedulers, 240->134s Track-10 win, CHAMP O(1) fork). The CATEGORICAL frame (Poly=propagators, C4.f phase-collapse witness, GoI=fixpoint) is ASPIRATIONAL/conjectural in the precise sense — substrate-first, theory-after. The novelty claims (FL-as-UTM, Poly=propagator) are positioning, not proven theorems.
- THE 'SUBSTRATE-FIRST, THEORY-AFTER' METHODOLOGY (VERIFIED — frames the whole corpus's epistemic posture). Project norm: build structures that don't yet have a closed categorical/algebraic theory; recognize the frame AFTERWARD in adjacent mathematics (owner-named precedent: logical resolution on propagator networks — no prior literature, structure ships, theory recognized later). Two consequences: (1) spine claims need not be Theorem-status before engineering proceeds; (2) the active-research FRONTIER is welcomed, not tolerated ('collaborators-via-engineering, not consumers waiting for theorems'). This is why the GBT-frame recognition (C1-C4) is framed as 'we built engineering that needed multiple algebraic frames, and now ONE frame [GBT semilattice enrichment] describes all of them.'
- FL(ℵ₀)-AS-UTM NOVELTY (VERIFIED, mechanically anchored). The specific framing — FL(ℵ₀) with Whitman's decision procedure as the foundational machine of PARALLEL computation, analogous to UTM for sequential — appears UNCLAIMED in print across a 2-round + patched audit. Nation-Paolini III (Nov 2025) proves Th(F_κ) undecidable for κ≥3 but the reduction is graph-theoretic (Nies 1996 ∃∀-theory of nice bipartite graphs), NOT Turing-direct — so the FL<->Turing bridge 'genuinely lives in OUR reading, not in the literature.' Whitman's embedding (F_κ ↪ F_3) is LOAD-BEARING inside the NP-III proof itself, a substantive modern appearance.
- POLY=PROPAGATOR NOVELTY (VERIFIED — the strongest mechanical negative result in the corpus). The identification of polynomial functors / dependent lenses with Sussman-Radul propagators on lattice-valued cells is DEFINITIVELY ABSENT from the canonical Niu-Spivak Poly text (full body + complete ~50-entry bibliography + complete index mechanically grepped: 0 hits for propagator/Sussman/Radul/lattice/TMS/Whitman). Closest kindred precedent: Spivak's AFOSR-2022 talk 'Is Poly the true language of computation?' (same Poly substrate, explicit Turing-successor rhetoric, but NO propagator identification). Radul's 2009 MIT thesis 'Propagation Networks: A Flexible and Expressive Substrate for Computation' is the direct ancestor for the 'substrate' framing (without the categorical identification).
- .pnet IS THE IR (VERIFIED, shipped — the lowering hinge). The propagator-network serialized state (.pnet) is consumed DIRECTLY by a thin BSP scheduler and lowered to LLVM (LoweredPNET via the Zig PoC); 'no separate IR phase is needed; the network state IS the IR' (engineering anchor A3 = the task's named anchor). Shipped facts: pnet-serialize.rkt, .pnet cache ON by default with --no-pnet-cache flag, 17 registries serialized, Track-10 PIR delivered 240->134s (44%) wall-time win + 1315 lines deleted. CHAMP structural sharing makes fork-prop-network O(1), which is what makes 'network-as-first-class-value' practically affordable.
- SCHEDULER PORTABILITY IS THE DEPLOYMENT THESIS (VERIFIED). Because the fixpoint is order-invariant, network state is scheduler-portable: a .pnet serialized by the Racket compiler can be rehydrated and run to the same fixpoint by ANY conforming scheduler — Gauss-Seidel, BSP, the out-of-tree Zig BSP PoC, or a future distributed runtime. This is the substrate-level LVars/CRDT guarantee lifted to the whole compiler. Outstanding gap (G2): the A/B harness currently compares WALL-TIME only; a fixpoint-IDENTITY assertion (same observable state across schedulers) is flagged but not yet added — though the CHANGELOG records owner judgment that equivalence was validated during PAR-1/BSP-2 and is citable from those PIRs.

**PReduce implications:**
- GBT (semilattice-enriched SMC) is the ADOPTED primary categorical frame, displacing Biondo M-adhesive. Substrate fit is DIRECT not approximate: 'our lattice-valued cells ARE the semilattices the enrichment requires.' BloomL's 3 monotone-merge axioms (commutative/associative/idempotent) are VERBATIM the first 3 GBT enrichment axioms; GBT's 4 distributivity axioms (composition+tensor over join) lift the lattice-level CALM claim to MORPHISM level. The e-class cell NTT declaration moves from ':lattice :structural :order :refinement' to ':lattice :structural :enrichment :semilattice' (+ ':Q-module Q' when cost is in scope); the merge function IS the semilattice join, e-class membership IS the join's existence.
- C3.e IS THE LOAD-BEARING ENGINEERING CLAIM: ONE residuation operator (signature \_Q : Q x M -> M, M = hom-sets C(A,B) in the enriched SMC) implements BOTH PReduce cost-guided extraction (residual against the cost-Q-module) AND Logic-Engine backward-chaining (residual against the propagator-Q-module). They differ only in the (Q,M) instantiation, not in operator code. Concrete merge target: unify PPN 4C Phase 1B's tropical-left-residual + Logic Engine backward-chaining against the C3.d signature. Falsification target: any operation semantically backward-chaining that doesn't fit the signature — if none surfaces in implementation, the merge is real code reduction.
- NAC support is a NEW first-class requirement for the rule format from day one (Tracks 0.2/0.3). Without negative application conditions, expansive rules (e.g. x -> (+ x 0)) don't terminate. NACs are needed in ANY categorical frame (Biondo OR GBT). The rule schema gets a first-class NAC field; serialized in the .pnet rule registry alongside rule-property tags and worldview-bitmask.
- Rule-property taxonomy reframes to ENRICHMENT-PRESERVING-OR-NOT tags mapping to strata + parallelism: IN-fragment (S0, Lévy-optimal sharing, HVM2-style); Adhesive-DPO (S0, DPOI confluence DECIDABLE for terminating systems per Bonchi et al. III MSCS 2022); Confluence-by-construction (S0, trivial parallelism); Non-monotone/retraction-eligible (S(-1), parallel-across-worldviews); Opaque FFI+effects (effect stratum). Track 3 ι-reduction moves from the OPEN M-adhesive frontier into the CLOSED EDPOI framework — critical-pair analysis is now well-defined and decidable.
- S(-1) retraction gets a precise categorical home: morphisms in a WORLDVIEW-PARAMETERIZED commutative-monoid-WITHOUT-idempotence enrichment, with ATMS supplying the missing coordination protocol (each ATMS branch = one enrichment specialization maintaining a specific assumption set). Parallel speculative evaluation = parallel maintenance of competing enrichment specializations; cost-bounded pruning = tropical-quantale residuation across worldviews; reconvergence = the LIMIT of the worldview-parameterized family back to the unparameterized S0 semilattice. This gives PReduce Master's 'two strata is enough' a categorical justification (two natural enrichment regimes).
- C2.f resolves a C1 open item and REDUCES engineering load: under Biondo M-adhesive, SRE Track 2D's guarantees had to be audited theorem-by-theorem for transfer to PReduce; under GBT both live in the SAME semilattice-enriched setting, so the transfer is a single SHARED-enrichment audit, not per-theorem.
- HVM2 (74,000 MIPS on RTX 4090) is the Track-2 β-reduction benchmark CEILING. β sits in the IN-fragment (interaction combinators = IN-fragment of GBT-enriched SMCs); CONJECTURE C4.e: the IN-fragment inherits HVM2-style massive parallelism + Lévy-optimal sharing automatically (shared subterm reduces at most once, via e-class cell union-find = HVM2's lazy-clone). Gap below HVM2 is engineering opportunity. This is speculative-bridging strength: the empirical implementation is the test.
- Track 5 persistence: the LOAD-BEARING object is the persistent REGISTRY OF OPTIMAL REWRITES (cost-extraction results keyed by the residuation problem), not just the persistent e-graph. Schema: key=(source-e-class-content-hash, cost-criterion-id, worldview-bitmask?), value=chosen extraction, regime-tag ∈ {ground/contextual/retraction-eligible/opaque}. Richer than Schlatt-2026 (which persists the residuation DOMAIN); this persists the residuation RESULTS, content-addressed by the QUESTION not the solution-space. Flagged as an externalizable publishable design point for A0/Poly.
- Kleene-star algorithms transfer WHOLESALE: Floyd-Warshall, Bellman-Ford, Dijkstra are all Kleene-star instances in different semirings (NICTA 2009 Isabelle/HOL formalization is a direct verification target). PReduce inherits these for fuel-cost propagation, all-pairs reduction-cost, critical-path analysis — the algorithmic library is decades old and well-formalized, no new algorithm needed.
- DGoIM (Muroya-Ghica 2017, same author lineage as GBT — Ghica is the G in TBGH) is the close engineering precedent for Track 6 speculative reduction: tokens=active propagators, graph-rewriting=rule firing, space-time-tradeoff-knob=cost-bounded speculation, call-by-need=retraction-stratum-aware reduction. Worth retrieving for the token-passing/rule-firing interleaving design.
- Track 9 NTT-typed rules: Prologos's QTT multiplicity discipline pushes the e-class subsystem into the NON-Cartesian monoidal setting (linear/affine restrict copy/delete). The GBT monoidal-not-just-Cartesian generalization 'isn't optional decoration — it's the right level for our substrate's eventual maturity.' NTT property declarations BECOME enrichment declarations (:linear/:affine/:unrestricted constrain the SMC structure). Bindings handled by Moss-Tiurin 2025 'E-Graphs With Bindings' (extends GBT to closed SMCs) — same framework, not separate work.

**.pnet/LLVM implications:**
- THE LOWERING THESIS (engineering anchor A3, the task's named anchor): '.pnet IS its own IR; the network state is the IR' — no tree/SSA IR construction phase. The Zig BSP scheduler PoC consumes serialized .pnet directly and lowers to LLVM via LoweredPNET. The lowering target is therefore a PROPAGATOR NETWORK (cells + propagators + scheduler), not an instruction stream — a network-as-IR rather than tree-as-IR or SSA-as-IR. A0 Q-A0-4 flags this as a strong claim needing novelty positioning vs equality-saturation-as-IR (Tate 2009) and e-graphs-as-IR.
- ENRICHMENT ANNOTATION must be a first-class .pnet cell-schema field VISIBLE TO THE LOWERING LAYER, because it DETERMINES THE PARALLELISM STRATEGY (engineering memo Track 0.3): ':semilattice' for S0; ':commutative-monoid' for S(-1); ':Q-module' with quantale-id for cost-bearing cells. Specifically: IN-fragment cells <-> HVM2-style massive parallelism; adhesive-DPO cells <-> DPOI critical-pair-aware scheduling. The collaborator's LLVM lowering reads this annotation to pick the lowering strategy per cell.
- SCHEDULER-PORTABLE .pnet is the cross-LANGUAGE lowering guarantee: because the fixpoint is order-invariant (H1), a .pnet serialized by the Racket compiler is rehydrated and run to the SAME fixpoint by the out-of-tree Zig scheduler in a different language — the LVars/CRDT order-independence lifted to the deployment artifact. Anchor A7 (queued) is the explicit cross-language round-trip durability test. This is the architectural precondition that makes LLVM lowering a SCHEDULER swap, not a recompile.
- CHAMP STRUCTURAL SHARING is the practical enabler: fork-prop-network is O(1) (commit 1462fd6), so treating the network as a first-class value (serialize/fork/rehydrate) is structural-sharing-O(1) not deep-copy-O(n). This is what makes .pnet-as-IR affordable at scale rather than merely conceptual.
- RUNTIME = GoI machine STRUCTURALLY (C4.c, Theorem-strength for the algebraic correspondence + Engineered-and-tested for the schedulers): run-to-quiescence IS the Kleene-star/trace computation. This explains WHY the Zig PoC ran reduction work AS propagation 'without category-shift' — there is no separate GoI interpreter to build for the lowered runtime; the propagator scheduler IS the machine. The LLVM-lowered runtime inherits this: it is a BSP scheduler over lowered cells/propagators, not a bespoke evaluator.
- ORTHOGONALITY CONSTRAINT (from .claude/rules — directly relevant to lowering): optimizations must live at the layer owning the concern (cell storage-strategy / propagator fire-pattern / scheduler). The Zig-LLVM scheduler is one of the named target schedulers (alongside Gauss-Seidel, BSP, future distributed); any cell/propagator optimization whose SEMANTICS depend on the scheduler breaks portability. The historical 'Option E BSP-aware piggyback' was REJECTED (2026-05-14) precisely because Gauss-Seidel and Zig-LLVM have no equivalent drain pass. Lowering must inherit, not re-derive, cell+propagator-level optimizations.
- OUTSTANDING LOWERING GAPS the corpus flags honestly: A3 'not yet frozen' (Zig PoC branch unpinned, lowering output not archived for a representative input — owner is 'not extremely interested' in pinning per G3); A7 cross-language round-trip durability test not yet built. So the LLVM-lowering claim is REAL (PoC exists per owner) but UNDER-INSTRUMENTED for paper-grade citation — aspirational-leaning relative to the well-anchored .pnet-cache-ships claim.
- REDUCTION-ON-NETWORK is the LARGEST remaining lowering piece: reduction.rkt is currently IMPERATIVE; PReduce lifts it entirely onto the network (e-graph + DPO + tropical-quantale + GoI) and 'retires reduction.rkt in its entirety.' Until that lands, the lowered runtime covers the compile-pipeline + the compile/runtime distinction UP TO evaluation but NOT evaluation itself — which is exactly why A0 is gated on PReduce (G1).

**Phase-collapse implications:**
- 'Collapse the phase problem' IS A0 headline H2, and the corpus's framing is precise and quotable: conventional compile-pipeline phases (parse/elaborate/type-check/resolve-constraints/retract/evaluate/cache) 'reduce to STRATUM BOUNDARIES on the same network, not separations between distinct artifacts.' The traditional multi-pass compiler becomes a SINGLE FIXPOINT computation with strata, where a 'phase boundary' is a stratification on one network. This directly answers the human's multi-pass-compiler-as-single-fixpoint question.
- THE CAUSAL MECHANISM (not metaphor): phase collapse is a CONSEQUENCE of deterministic parallelism (H1). Chain: lattice cells + monotone propagators + semilattice merge => order-invariant fixpoint => scheduler-portable state => .pnet-as-IR => no preferred phase ordering => no phase distinction => collapse. The phases collapse BECAUSE there is no order they must run in — if firing order doesn't change the fixpoint, the network can be shared between compile-time elaboration and run-time evaluation, serialized, and rehydrated by any scheduler. The phase boundary HAS no semantic content to preserve.
- SEED EVIDENCE (E1, the strongest direct support): 'S0's fiber has expanded to include resolution — the phase boundary between type inference and constraint resolution has DISSOLVED within S0' (2026-03-22 categorical-foundations note §4.3). The dissolution is already SHIPPED for at least one phase pair (type-inference <-> constraint-resolution), observed first as a categorical fact about the fibration structure, then recognized as the phase-collapse seed.
- CATEGORICAL WITNESS (C4.f, structural conjecture, load-bearing): the SAME Kleene-star/trace operator fires on typing cells at compile time and reduction cells at run time — 'the algorithm is invariant; only the cells differ.' If this holds (pending implementation discipline of typing-cell + reduction-cell on the same scheduler), it is the categorical fact UNDERNEATH the A0 H2 headline. The phase distinction collapses because both 'phases' are the same trace/fixpoint operator over a traced semilattice-enriched SMC, distinguished only by cell contents.
- COMPILE-TIME = RUN-TIME via SHARED ENGINE (E2): 'Prologos requires unification in two contexts: the type checker (at compile time) and the runtime logic engine (at runtime)' — same machine, two phases. The dependent-typing phase-blur (E8: 'type checking value-dependent protocols may require evaluating functions at type-checking time, blurring the phase distinction') is turned from a BLUR into a clean COLLAPSE: unlike Idris/Lean which REUSE the type checker as a runtime evaluator, the LHC 'handles it by NOT HAVING a separate runtime evaluator at all.'
- SCOPE HONESTY — THE PREDUCE CAVEAT (G1, the single sharpest aspirational-vs-rigorous boundary): reduction is the ONE place phase collapse is not yet complete. reduction.rkt is currently imperative; until PReduce lifts evaluation onto the network, A0 must EITHER wait for PReduce OR scope-limit phase collapse to 'compile-pipeline phases + the compile/runtime distinction up to evaluation.' Owner chose to WAIT (~6+ months, possibly close to fully-self-hosting) for the strongest version covering reduction. So the FULL phase-collapse claim is currently ASPIRATIONAL pending PReduce; the PARTIAL claim (everything except evaluation) is rigorous and shipped.
- RIGOR ASSESSMENT: phase collapse for compile-pipeline phases is WELL-SUPPORTED (E1-E6 cite shipped engineering: .pnet serialization, 17 registries, CHAMP O(1) fork, Track-10 win). Phase collapse INCLUDING reduction is DESIGN-COMPLETE but not implemented (E7 = PReduce planned). The precise statement itself is 'composed from corpus evidence rather than copied from any single source' and is FLAGGED FOR OWNER VALIDATION — i.e., the project has DONE the thing across phases but had not NAMED the thing as one isolated claim until this audit. That naming gap is exactly what A0 fills.
- PROVOCATIVE WORKING TITLES the corpus floats: 'A Propagator-Network Compiler with No Phases'; 'Compilation as Fixpoint'; 'Compile-Time and Run-Time as the Same Network' — these encode the phase-collapse thesis as the paper's identity. A standalone Phase Collapse conceptual paper is a registered future option (decision deferred to post-A0-draft) if the claim outgrows what A0 can carry.

**Non-vN reach implications:**
- The programme is squarely a NON-von-Neumann reach: the central move is to displace the single-computer-with-global-memory image with a propagator network of 'local, independent, stateless machines interconnected with stateful storage cells' (Radul's framing, cited as direct ancestor). Computation is information-flow-through-cells, not instruction-stream-over-memory.
- Valiant's BSP is explicitly positioned as the VON NEUMANN analogue ('bridging model'), and the corpus deliberately CONTRASTS with it: BSP is engineering-universal (compilable from many languages to many machines) but NOT computability-universal. The LHC instead aims at the order-theoretic/algebraic substrate (FL(ℵ₀)) as the computability-level foundation — a different axis than Valiant's hardware-software bridge.
- FL(ℵ₀)-as-UTM is framed as the PARALLEL analogue of the UTM for SEQUENTIAL computation, explicitly distinguished from the von-Neumann/RAM lineage (PRAM is dismissed as 'RAM in parallel'). The substrate-vs-decider posture (Q-B1) is the key non-vN formalization gap: substrate = (FL(ℵ₀), Whitman decision procedure, Poly-shaped propagators, BSP-style scheduler), compared structurally to UTM = (tape, transition function, head, trivial scheduler) — a deliberately non-von-Neumann machine model.
- The 'all-at-once, all in parallel, structurally emergent information flow ON-NETWORK' design mantra (the project's gravity) is the operational anti-thesis of von-Neumann sequencing: ordering must EMERGE from dataflow depth, never from installation sequence or imperative dispatch. for/fold over independent items is flagged as 'step-think.' This is the non-vN discipline enforced at every keystroke.
- Distributed/non-vN reach is structurally built-in (E12): 'a distributed propagator network is the same network partitioned across nodes, where boundary cells are replicated and synchronized via lattice merge. Because merge is a semilattice operation, message delivery order doesn't matter — strong eventual consistency BY CONSTRUCTION.' The CRDT/CALM substrate makes the leap from single-node scheduler-order-independence to multi-node message-order-independence FREE.
- Honest limit: the corpus does NOT claim Church-Turing-shape equivalence for the parallel substrate. Paper B framing was deliberately SOFTENED from 'is' to 'candidate / analogy / research programme' (registered decision 2026-05-08); the owner said 'more pieces in play before we can grapple with that claim' (Q-B4). So the non-vN universality claim is ASPIRATIONAL and explicitly hedged — the rigorous content is the empirical witness (LHC ships) + the prior-art gap (nobody else framed FL-as-parallel-UTM), not a proven equivalence.

**Novelty / prior art:**
- NOVEL (mechanically anchored): Polynomial functors / dependent lenses = the categorical identity of Sussman-Radul propagators on lattice-valued cells. ABSENT from the entire Niu-Spivak Poly text (body + ~50-entry bibliography + complete index: 0 hits). Absent from surveyed Topos blog/Spivak-talk corpus and from the propagator-network literature. This is 'the strongest possible mechanical negative result.' Closest kindred rhetoric: Spivak AFOSR-2022 'Is Poly the true language of computation?' (same substrate, no propagator link).
- NOVEL (positioning, not proven): FL(ℵ₀)+Whitman as a UTM-analogue for PARALLEL computation. Unclaimed in print. The FL<->Turing bridge lives in OUR reading: Nation-Paolini III undecidability reduces from Nies-1996 bipartite-graph ∃∀-undecidability (graph-theoretic, NOT Turing-direct), so the literature gives the algebra but never the computational framing.
- DIRECT ANCESTOR / must-cite: Radul, 'Propagation Networks: A Flexible and Expressive Substrate for Computation' (MIT 2009) — 'Substrate' is literally in the title; abstract proposes 'a shift in the foundations of computation'; cells store 'everything you know about a value' with accumulating (join-semilattice) merge. Any propagator-base universality claim MUST cite this for the 'substrate' framing while distinguishing the KIND of substrate (algebraic FL vs architectural propagator-network).
- DIRECT LINEAGE (owner-attested, promoted from 'adjacent' to 'direct'): Kuper LVars (lattice-based deterministic parallelism) + Hellerstein-Alvaro CALM + Conway et al. BloomL. Owner: 'our efforts stem from this, directly inspired from this work.' A0 acknowledgments credit Kuper/Hellerstein-Alvaro/BloomL/Sussman-Radul explicitly. CALM has been an iff-THEOREM since Ameloot et al. 2013 (JACM) in the relational-transducer model — our corpus uses it correctly as theorem-status.
- CLOSEST COMPETING UTM-ANALOGUE RHETORIC to engage head-on: Hewitt actors ('universal primitives of concurrent digital computation', 1973-2010 — different substrate, concurrent message-passing) and Spivak Poly-AFOSR-2022 (same Poly substrate, explicit Turing/von-Neumann successor claim, Turing-machines-in-Poly demonstrated). These two are the priority-rhetoric precedents Paper B must position against.
- DROPPED FROM SCOPE (owner direction): Wolfram's multicomputational paradigm (2021, ruliad, multiway/hypergraph rewriting). Factually it's the most aggressive competing universality framing ('fourth paradigm', 'foundation', applied to distributed computing) and structurally adjacent (multiway graphs have 'the mathematical structure of Hasse diagrams'), but owner: 'I deem it irrelevant to our efforts.' Treat as a one-paragraph competing-paradigm note at most.
- PRIOR-ART for the PReduce categorical frame (load-bearing, external): TBGH/Tiurin-Barrett-Ghica-Hu 'Equivalence Hypergraphs: DPO Rewriting for Monoidal E-Graphs' (LICS 2025, arXiv:2406.15882) — the semilattice-enriched-SMC frame, Thm 6.5 full-completeness quotient construction. Russo 2010 Q-module residuation API. Bonchi et al. III (MSCS 2022) DPOI confluence decidability. Moss-Tiurin 2025 E-Graphs With Bindings. These are ADOPTED frames, not novelty claims — the corpus is honest that the synthesis (recognizing OUR substrate in them) is the contribution.
- NOVELTY-POSITIONING AUDITS still OWED before paper work (corpus is disciplined about this): Q-A1 (LRP novelty vs classical stratified-semantics — Apt-Blair-Walker, Przymusinski, Van Gelder-Ross-Schlipf, Gelfond-Lifschitz; nobody has unified NAF+WF+type+effect+retraction+topology under one structural framework, but this needs defending); Q-Poly-1 (Poly=propagator vs dependent-optics literature — Hedges/Myers/Capucci/Milewski); Q-Poly-2 (Galois-bridges-between-cell-domains vs Cousot-Cousot abstract-interpretation Galois connections). The corpus does NOT yet claim these are won.
- HONEST CAVEAT on the audit's own reach: 'does not establish the FL-as-UTM claim is CORRECT — only that it is UNCLAIMED.' The claim would still need to specify (i) the encoding from Turing tapes/parallel programs into FL(ℵ₀) words (the missing structural step NP-III implicates but doesn't exhibit), (ii) which Whitman-decidable subset plays 'halting/accepting', (iii) what notion of universality, (iv) why preferable to actor/Kahn/LVar foundations.

**Open questions:**
- Q-A0-1/Q-A0-2 are resolved at DRAFT level only — the precise phase-collapse and scheduler-portability STATEMENTS are 'composed from corpus evidence' and FLAGGED FOR OWNER VALIDATION against project intent. They are not yet owner-locked.
- G1 (the gating decision): does A0 wait for PReduce (chosen, ~6+ months) or scope-limit phase collapse to exclude reduction? The FULL phase-collapse claim (including evaluation) is design-complete but UNIMPLEMENTED — reduction.rkt is still imperative. This is the single largest aspirational-vs-shipped gap in the whole programme.
- G2: the A/B scheduler harness compares WALL-TIME only; a fixpoint-IDENTITY assertion (same observable state across schedulers, not just same runtime) is flagged but not added. CHANGELOG records owner judgment it was validated during PAR-1/BSP-2 and is citable from those PIRs — but no standalone same-fixpoint cross-scheduler test exists. INFERRED: this is a real instrumentation gap for paper-grade H1 evidence.
- G3: the Zig BSP scheduler PoC + LLVM lowering branch is UNPINNED and unarchived; owner is 'not extremely interested' in pinning it. So the .pnet->LoweredPNET->LLVM lowering claim (anchor A3) rests on owner attestation, not a frozen artifact — under-instrumented for citation.
- C4.f (phase-collapse categorical witness) is a STRUCTURAL CONJECTURE pending 'implementation discipline of typing-cell + reduction-cell on the same scheduler.' Until PReduce puts reduction cells on the network, the 'same Kleene-star operator on different cell contents' claim cannot be operationally exhibited.
- C4.d: does the semilattice-enriched SMC actually support a FULL trace operator (Joyal-Street-Verity / Haghverdi-Scott)? The construction 'should follow standard categorical-GoI but isn't literally in the published literature for the semilattice-enriched specialization.' First proof obligation; currently structural conjecture.
- C2.e/C2.c: is critical-pair-absence EXACTLY join-well-definedness on parallel morphisms (the cleanest falsification target)? And does the CALM-monotone => semilattice-enriched-model CONVERSE hold for the rewriting setting (speculative bridging, unproved)?
- Whitman-from-recursive-merge (Q-B3): does the recursive-on-outermost-operator merge shape PROVE Whitman's condition (W) or merely correlate with it (10/10 empirically)? Anchors Paper B's empirical pillar; needs Nation consultation. Plus the binder boundary (Pi/Sigma/lambda breaks distributivity) — is it a known model-theoretic phenomenon or a separate object of study (Q-C2)?
- Substrate-vs-decider formalization (Q-B1, blocking for Paper B): how to formalize substrate=(FL, Whitman-procedure, Poly-propagators, BSP-scheduler) so reviewers don't read FL-as-UTM as a category error? Required before Paper B writing; Hewitt-actor-precedent study needed.
- S6-Q1: does ATMS label discipline lift CLEANLY to enrichment-parameterization, or is a 'quasi-enrichment' notion needed for S(-1)? S6-Q2: relation to colored e-graphs (Singher-Itzhaky 2023) — direct comparison target, unresolved.
- Q-A0-4 (.pnet-IS-IR novelty): what other compilers have network-as-IR rather than tree/SSA-as-IR? Equality-saturation/e-graphs-as-IR (Tate 2009) is named but a positioning audit is owed before the claim can be asserted as novel.
- INFERRED gap: the corpus is internally consistent but the OWNER-VALIDATION step is pending across A0's two headlines AND the precise phase-collapse statement. Multiple key claims (C4.f, the precise H2 statement, the 4-scheduler equivalence) are at 'draft/conjecture, owner to validate' — the synthesis is mature but not yet ratified.

Grades the Hyperlattice Conjecture (Hasse diagram = optimal parallel decomposition) BY LATTICE VARIETY: Boolean CONFIRMED (Hasse(B_n)=hypercube graph), distributive STRONG (Garg LLP), series-parallel CONFIRMED, modular/SD/free NO published parallel claim. §4.3 universal-across-varieties form = APPARENT NOVELTY; §4.5 graded form = robust fallback. §1 two-mind-space framing (algorithmic-thinking ↔ information-flow correspondence). §6.4 explicitly: SH's super-optimization claim rests on parallel-decomposition optimality — with HC it becomes 'provably Hasse-decomposition-optimal,' not just empirically fast.
- (our-doc) .claude/rules/structural-thinking.md — Project rule (loaded in context). §Hyperlattice Conjecture: two claims — (1) universality every computation → fixpoint on lattices; (2) optimality the Hasse diagram IS the optimal parallel decomposition. Q6 (Hasse diagram) = 'most important' SRE lens question. 'This is not a metaphor. It is a structural identity.' The design mantra is the operational form of the conjecture.
- (our-doc) docs/tracking/2026-04-30_SH_MASTER.md — SH-series master tracker (confirmation read). Confirms two-axis self-hosting framing; PReduce/Track 9 is a CROSS-SERIES dependency (not an SH track) that SH consumes and waits on for the super-optimization claim; .pnet round-trip is the deployment-artifact linchpin; Track 4 (production LLVM substrate) = ⬜ no code.

**Key findings:**
- VERIFIED — The central internal claim (line 107): 'the propagator network is itself the intermediate representation. There is no high-level IR + optimization framework on top stack; there is one substrate, with cells holding lattice values and propagators implementing the lattice merges. Typing rules, reduction rules, optimization rules, and code-generation rules all fire on the same network as propagators.' This IS the phase-collapse thesis: the four classically-distinct compiler phases collapse onto ONE network because they are all the same kind of object (a propagator firing).
- VERIFIED — The MLIR-at-runtime-granularity analogy (line 109, verbatim): 'This is structurally the same move MLIR made (multi-level IR with dialects) but at runtime granularity rather than at static-pass granularity. Our dialects are SRE-registered domains; our passes are propagator firings; our lowering is a stratified scheduler choosing which propagators fire when. The architecture is born super-optimizing because the optimization is the same kind of thing as the typing.' The 3-way correspondence dialects=SRE-domains / passes=firings / lowering=stratified-scheduler is the precise internal framing the facet asks for.
- VERIFIED — The honest thesis disclaimer is stated twice (§1 line 20, §6.executive): 'The thesis is not we'll be faster than LLVM.' The distinctiveness claim is COMBINATORIAL: the dimensions (correctness via category theory, cost-weighted lattice merges, provenance typecheck→runtime, content-addressed artifacts) are ones the existing literature approached ONE AT A TIME; Prologos's architecture happens to put them on the SAME substrate (§4 table: 'The literature picks two or three; Prologos's architecture, if executed, picks all twelve').
- VERIFIED — 'Equality saturation happens automatically when cells are tropical-quantale-valued and propagators implement rewriting rules. No separate engine' (§3.2, line 121). The classic egg/egglog saturate-then-extract-by-cost is reframed as substrate-intrinsic: extraction is the MERGE OPERATION, not a separate post-saturation pass. This is the operational core of phase-collapse for the optimization phase specifically.
- VERIFIED — The compiler-IS-a-fixpoint-engine claim is grounded in deeper theory across the companions: Module Theory §12 states 'Any computation is a fixpoint of an endomorphism on a module over a lattice-ordered ring' (refining the Hyperlattice Conjecture); Categorical Foundations frames run-to-quiescence as the fixpoint and §9.4 frames e-graph saturation as a colimit over rewrite rules. The fixpoint-engine framing is theory-load-bearing, not rhetorical.
- VERIFIED — §3.7 'Stratified rewriting subsumes pass ordering': pass ordering (a perennial LLVM/MLIR problem) is REPLACED by lattice structure — 'monotone optimizations live at S0, non-monotone at higher strata. There's no is this pass before or after that pass? question — it's what stratum does this rule live in? and the answer is determined by the rule's algebraic properties.' This is the phase-collapse claim applied to the SCHEDULING dimension: ordering EMERGES from algebra rather than being configured.
- VERIFIED — The grounded/designed/speculative boundary (§6) is drawn honestly and is the facet's requested 'honest boundary.' GROUNDED today: network-IS-IR (PPN Tracks 1-2 done, Track 4 in flight), e-graphs-are-adhesive (external Biondo et al. CALCO 2025), no-critical-pairs (prop:ctor-desc-tag, SRE Track 2 PIR), ATMS provenance (atms.rkt 877 lines), .pnet exists (~735ms vs ~20s cold), QTT erasure (qtt.rkt 2462 lines). DESIGNED-NOT-DELIVERED: Track 9/PReduce tropical-cost reduction + e-graph saturation (Stage 1 research, design pending), LLVM substrate (no code), .pnet propagator-structure round-trip, cell-level incrementality. SPECULATIVE: that tropical-on-cells delivers competitive super-optimization IN PRACTICE; that the combination meaningfully OUTPERFORMS the literature on ≥1 dimension; that HVM2's order-independence carries over (propagator networks are MORE GENERAL than interaction nets — 'might or might not carry; need to verify'); that .pnet content-addressing scales.
- VERIFIED — PReduce/Track 9 is the explicit LINCHPIN that converts 'compiler' → 'super-optimizing compiler' (§5, §8). It is what lands the four sub-claims simultaneously: reduction-as-DPO (adhesive guarantees) + tropical-on-cells (cost-driven extraction) + e-graph saturation as a consequence + interaction-net parallelism + GoI/DGoIM evaluation semantics. SH master confirms PReduce is a cross-series dependency SH consumes; 'SH tracks gate behind PReduce delivery for the super-optimization story to land' (§8, line 280).
- INFERRED: The phase-collapse is genuinely DIFFERENT FROM e-graph-as-MLIR-dialect (DialEgg/eqsat) precisely because the latter is 'bolted on (separate dialect, separate engine)' (§2.1) whereas the Prologos claim is that there is no separate engine AT ALL — the substrate that does typing IS the substrate that does optimization. The novelty is the ABSENCE of a phase boundary, not a better phase-crossing mechanism. (Deduced from §3.1's contrast list: egg→MLIR, Souper→LLVM, Halide→ML-cost-model, HVM→separate-runtime, all 'bolted onto' a host.)
- VERIFIED — The Hasse-optimal-decomposition material (structural-thinking.md + the Parallel-Decomposition note) UNDERWRITES the parallel/native targets and is what would lift the super-optimization claim from EMPIRICAL to STRUCTURAL. The note states explicitly (§6.4): without the parallel-decomposition optimality argument, 'super-optimizing compiler is empirical, not structural. With it, there's a path to provable optimality.' The optimality claim is graded: CONFIRMED for Boolean (Hasse=hypercube), STRONG for distributive (Garg LLP), APPARENT NOVELTY for the universal-across-varieties form.

**PReduce implications:**
- PReduce is the load-bearing track for the ENTIRE super-optimization thesis — §5/§8 make it the linchpin that converts 'self-hosted competitive language' into 'self-hosted super-optimizing compiler.' Without PReduce, the phase-collapse story is structurally true for typing/elaboration (PPN) but the OPTIMIZATION phase has nothing to collapse onto the network. Prioritization is explicit: SH tracks gate behind PReduce.
- The intended PReduce architecture is fully prefigured by Module Theory §6: each rewrite rule = a propagator; cells hold e-class values (sets of equivalent terms); merge = set-union (monotone, CALM-safe); BSP fires all applicable rewrites per round; extraction uses tropical semiring. Module theory GUARANTEES quotient well-definedness (rewrite rules respect congruence), saturation termination (Noetherian if rewrites terminate), and unique extraction optimum (if cost is a semiring valuation). This is the design recipe PReduce should implement.
- The cost lattice is an OPEN DESIGN QUESTION (§7 Q1): tropical (min-plus) is the obvious Viterbi-cheapest-derivation choice, but the quantale framework admits richer COMBINED cost lattices (instruction-count × code-size × power × latency). The Parallel-Decomposition note §6.2 Example 3 grounds the tropical-fuel cell as a Lawvere quantale on [0,∞] with Bellman-Ford/Floyd-Warshall structural parallelism. PReduce must pick its cost structure deliberately — the algebra is given, the combination is the design choice.
- CRITICAL VERIFICATION TARGET for PReduce: §6 SPECULATIVE entry — 'that the parallel-evaluation properties HVM2 demonstrates carry over directly to our substrate. Interaction nets are a specific algebraic structure; propagator networks are MORE GENERAL. The order-independence might or might not carry; need to verify.' PReduce should NOT assume reduction-on-propagators inherits interaction-net beta-optimality / order-independence for free; this is an empirical question to settle early, since §5 leans on it for the massively-parallel claim.
- Dependent-typing × equality-saturation is flagged as an open frontier (§7 Q2, citing Moss 2025 'E-Graphs with Bindings' arXiv:2505.00807). PReduce operates in a dependently-typed context where binding structure complicates rewriting. This is research territory, not engineering — PReduce's e-graph must handle binders (the Categorical Foundations §9.1 dependent-polynomial-functor open question is the same issue from the typing side).
- Categorical Foundations §9.4 frames the e-graph layer's interaction with the polynomial-functor structure as OPEN: 'e-classes are polynomial functors with position equivalence (positions in the same e-class are identified), and saturation is the colimit over rewrite rules.' If PReduce wants the NTT typing discipline to apply to e-class cells, this colimit-vs-polynomial-functor compatibility needs working out.
- PReduce's extraction is itself a Hasse-driven parallel decomposition on a quotient module (Parallel-Decomposition §6.3): tropical cost gives each e-class a position in the cost lattice; extraction = walk the Hasse adjacency of the cost lattice; the optimal extraction algorithm IS the Hasse-walk for the tropical variety. This directly connects PReduce's algorithmic foundation to the phase-collapse + Hasse-optimality story.
- INFERRED: PReduce sits under PRN (per SH master line 61: 'PReduce sits under PRN as PRN's reduction application'), so its theory home is Module Theory + the adhesive-categories note. The reduction-as-DPO-rewriting grounding (adhesive guarantees, no critical pairs) is what gives PReduce confluence-by-construction — meaning PReduce can do EAGER parallel rewriting WITHOUT Cranelift's acyclic-e-graph correctness compromise (§3.3).

**.pnet/LLVM implications:**
- .pnet round-trip of propagator structure is the SUBSTRATE ENABLER for two of the seven distinctiveness claims (§3.4 provenance-into-runtime, §3.6 mobility/content-addressing). Currently GROUNDED: .pnet exists for module-level caching (~735ms load vs ~20s cold). DESIGNED-NOT-DELIVERED: '.pnet doesn't currently round-trip propagator structure' (§6). This is SH master's explicit linchpin (Track 1) — until .pnet is a network-as-VALUE, the mobile/content-addressed/provenance-carrying claims are aspirational.
- The content-addressing claim is STRONGER than IPVM/Fission deterministic-Wasm hashing (§3.6): 'Two .pnet files with the same hash are not just bit-equal — they're fixpoint-equal with the same derivation tree.' This is a fixpoint-semantic equivalence (the network as a value carries its ATMS provenance), not a syntactic one. SPECULATIVE caveat (§6): whether the full propagator-network value 'hashes cleanly at scale' is empirical — CHAMP scales, but the whole-network hash is unproven at scale.
- LLVM is the CEILING-RAISING work, NOT the distinctiveness work. §8: 'Track 4 (production LLVM substrate) is the ceiling-raising work. After Track 9 + Track 1, native compilation removes the constant-factor handicap.' The architecture's distinctiveness (the phase-collapse, the combined dimensions) is independent of LLVM; LLVM only removes the Racket constant-factor. SH master confirms Track 4 = ⬜ no code, gated on Track 9 + Track 1.
- OPEN DESIGN QUESTION on the IR tower (§7 Q8 + SH master Q2): 'Is there any value in adding a static lowering tower between propagator network and LLVM IR, or is the substrate-direct approach sufficient?' Because the substrate is dialect-FREE at runtime (lattice domains ARE the dialects), the classic MLIR n-layer dialect tower may be unnecessary — SH master line 94 notes the lower-IR-layer count 'depends on Track 9 (PReduce) outputs — if super-optimization happens at the propagator-network layer, the lower IR can be thinner.' Phase-collapse at the network layer would justify a THIN lowering path to LLVM.
- The Cell/Propagator/Scheduler orthogonality rule (on-network.md, loaded in context) is the LOAD-BEARING constraint for any LLVM-substrate optimization: optimizations declared at cell + propagator level work under ANY scheduler (Gauss-Seidel, BSP, Zig-LLVM, distributed). This is explicitly future-track guidance: 'Without the orthogonality discipline, optimizations would couple to the current scheduler (Racket BSP) and have to be re-derived for each future scheduler.' The Zig-LLVM scheduler is named as a target the orthogonality must survive.
- INFERRED: The 'lowering = stratified scheduler' half of the MLIR analogy implies LLVM codegen is itself a stratum/propagator-class, NOT a separate backend pass. If taken literally, the native-code-emission rules would be propagators firing at a codegen stratum on the same network — extending phase-collapse all the way to codegen. The doc states codegen rules 'fire on the same network as propagators' (§3.1) but provides no concrete codegen-propagator design (it is in the DESIGNED/SPECULATIVE tier). Flagged as the most architecturally ambitious and least-grounded part of the thesis.
- Incremental compilation falls out of the substrate 'for free' (§3.5): 'We don't need a Salsa-style query system; we have one for free in the substrate.' CHAMP persistent structures preserve unchanged sub-network state; BSP worklist firing is demand-driven recomputation. This is a structural property the LLVM substrate INHERITS — but §6 flags cell-level incrementality as DESIGNED-NOT-DELIVERED ('substrate supports it naturally; no engineering yet'). The native runtime must reimplement CHAMP + BSP (SH master) to keep this.

**Phase-collapse implications:**
- THE MECHANISM (verbatim, §3.1 line 107): the four classically-separate compiler phases — typing, reduction, optimization, code-generation — COLLAPSE onto one propagator network because each is realized as a propagator firing, and 'the optimization is the same kind of thing as the typing.' There is no high-level-IR + optimization-framework-on-top stack; one substrate (cells holding lattice values, propagators implementing lattice merges) does all four. This is the precise internal claim the facet requests.
- 'PASSES ARE FIRINGS' is the compression of the MLIR-at-runtime-granularity analogy (§3.1 line 109): dialects=SRE-registered-domains, passes=propagator-firings, lowering=stratified-scheduler-choosing-which-propagators-fire-when. The shift from STATIC-PASS granularity (MLIR/LLVM run discrete passes in a configured pipeline) to RUNTIME granularity (the network reaches fixpoint; firings interleave as dataflow dictates) is the whole content of phase-collapse. A pass is no longer a phase you schedule; it is a propagator that fires when its inputs are ready.
- Phase-ORDERING is dissolved, not solved (§3.7). The perennial 'is pass A before or after pass B?' question is REPLACED by 'what stratum does this rule live in?', and the stratum is determined by the rule's ALGEBRAIC PROPERTIES (monotone → S0; non-monotone → higher stratum; topology-changing → topology stratum). Ordering EMERGES from lattice algebra, not pipeline configuration. This is the design-mantra's 'structurally emergent... ordering must EMERGE from dataflow depth, not from installation sequence' applied to compiler phases.
- The fixpoint-engine framing is what MAKES the collapse coherent: the compiler is not a sequence of transformations but a single monotone-fixpoint computation (run-to-quiescence). Module Theory §12 gives the precise form ('a fixpoint of an endomorphism on a module over a lattice-ordered ring'); Categorical Foundations frames quiescence as the fixpoint and saturation as a colimit. The phases collapse because they are all sub-computations OF THE SAME FIXPOINT, distinguished only by which cells/propagators/strata they involve.
- Phase-collapse is grounded UNEVENLY across phases — this is the honest boundary. Typing/elaboration collapse is GROUNDED (PPN Tracks 1-2 done; the elaborator already runs on the propagator network). Reduction collapse is DESIGNED-NOT-DELIVERED (Track 9/PReduce — reduction.rkt is still an imperative walk, named as the Track 9 target in SH master). Optimization collapse is DESIGNED (depends on PReduce's tropical-cost cells). Codegen collapse is the most SPECULATIVE (no concrete codegen-propagator design exists). So 'all four phases fire on one network' is an ARCHITECTURAL ENDPOINT, not a current fact — the doc is explicit that this is Stage 0/1, no design commitments.
- CRITICAL ADVERSARIAL NOTE: phase-collapse is the architectural claim PPN Track 4 is specifically at risk of FAILING to deliver — the workflow.md 'Network Reality Check' rule (loaded in context) was codified precisely because 'PPN Track 4 diverged from its D.3 design: three critique rounds validated a propagator-native architecture, but implementation built function-call wrappers around the same imperative infer.' The phase-collapse thesis is only TRUE if the rules are genuine net-add-propagator calls writing results via net-cell-write, not hash-table-dispatched function calls that merely match the imperative result. The thesis is the vision; the Network Reality Check is the gate that prevents claiming it without building it.
- Phase-collapse subsumes the equality-saturation engine boundary specifically (§3.2): the egg/egglog 'saturate then extract-by-cost' two-phase shape collapses to ONE merge operation when cells are tropical-quantale-valued — 'the cost function is not a separate post-saturation extraction — it's the merge operation.' This is the single cleanest worked instance of phase-collapse: an entire external engine's two-phase protocol becomes one cell-merge.
- INFERRED: The phase-collapse is the COMPILER-SIDE dual of the runtime-side claim that reduction-on-propagators inherits interaction-net order-independence. Both are 'the substrate does the work that elsewhere requires a dedicated engine/pass.' The internal coherence of the thesis depends on BOTH dualities holding; §6 flags the runtime-side one as SPECULATIVE (propagator networks more general than interaction nets), which transitively weakens confidence in the parallelism dividend of the compiler-side collapse until PReduce verifies it.

**Non-vN reach implications:**
- The Hyperlattice Conjecture's OPTIMALITY claim is what underwrites parallel (non-von-Neumann / massively-parallel) targets: 'the Hasse diagram of the lattice IS the optimal parallel decomposition' (structural-thinking.md). For the super-optimization thesis this is the bridge from 'fast in practice' to 'structurally optimal' — Parallel-Decomposition §6.4 states it directly: with HC, SH delivers 'a compiler whose architectural endpoint is provably optimal at the substrate level — not just empirically fast, but Hasse-decomposition-optimal.'
- The optimality claim is GRADED BY VARIETY (Parallel-Decomposition §4.2), which is the honest non-VN-reach boundary: Boolean = CONFIRMED textbook (Hasse(B_n) = hypercube graph; hypercube algorithms ARE the optimal parallel patterns — Akl 1989, Leighton 1991), already exploited in the ATMS worldview space; distributive = STRONG GRADED PRECEDENT (Garg LLP, SPAA 2020 — Hasse poset IS the parallel decomposition for lattice-linear predicates); series-parallel = CONFIRMED classical scheduling; modular / semidistributive / free = NO PUBLISHED PARALLEL CLAIM. The parallel-reach guarantee is therefore strongest exactly where the compiler's hottest lattices live (Boolean worldview space, distributive type-equality).
- The UNIVERSAL-across-varieties form of the conjecture is APPARENT NOVELTY (Parallel-Decomposition §4.3, §7.3) and is what would make the super-optimization claim a genuine theorem rather than a per-case observation. Four specifically-novel sub-claims: (1) parallel decomposition is UNIQUELY DETERMINED by lattice variety (structural, not a design choice); (2) holds for EVERY variety, not just well-studied ones; (3) substrate-level realization where each cell's variety drives its parallel-algorithm anchor automatically; (4) SELF-HOSTING CLOSURE — the compiler that compiles Hasse-driven programs is itself a Hasse-driven network (Garg has no analog). #4 is the reach claim unique to the super-optimization thesis.
- The broadcast propagator is the operationalized non-VN primitive: it IS Blelloch parallel-prefix-scan IS butterfly IS embedded-hypercube (Parallel-Decomposition §2.4), with measured A/B speedups (2.3× at N=3, 75.6× at N=100 per BSP-LE Track 2). This is the EMPIRICAL anchor that the Hasse-adjacency theory predicts — 'the polynomial functor made operational' (Categorical Foundations + on-network.md). The super-optimizing compiler inherits this for any embarrassingly-parallel sub-computation (e-graph rewrites, multi-candidate readiness, union-branch forks).
- Two FALSIFIABILITY questions gate the universal reach claim (Parallel-Decomposition §4.4), both externally tractable: (1) has anyone claimed Hasse(arbitrary lattice) = optimal parallel decomposition in universal form? (2) is there a counterexample — a fixpoint where the optimal parallel algorithm uses a DIFFERENT graph than the Hasse diagram? If (1) returns nothing the universal claim is novel; if (2) returns a counterexample the conjecture is false universally and retreats to the graded form (which is the robust core). The super-optimization thesis is SAFE under the graded fallback — its strongest lattices (Boolean, distributive) are precisely the confirmed/strong cases.
- INFERRED: 'non-von-Neumann reach' for this facet means the architecture is targetable at GPU/manycore/distributed substrates because the parallel decomposition is a STRUCTURAL property of the lattice, not bolted-on parallelization. HVM2 (§2.4: 400 MIPS→74,000 MIPS RTX-4090) is the named exemplar of how far interaction-net-style order-independence reaches on a GPU; the thesis claims propagator networks reach similarly IF the order-independence carries (flagged SPECULATIVE in §6). The Cell/Propagator/Scheduler orthogonality rule is the discipline that keeps the decomposition portable across these non-VN schedulers.
- OPEN QUESTION bearing on reach (Parallel-Decomposition §8.1 Q1): is CALM ('monotone ⇒ coordination-free', correctness) the SAME theorem as the Hyperlattice optimality claim ('Hasse adjacency = optimal decomposition', shape) in different vocabularies, or do they LAYER (CALM = correctness floor; HC = optimality ceiling)? The super-optimization thesis needs the layering reading — CALM guarantees the parallel execution is CORRECT regardless of schedule; HC would additionally guarantee a chosen schedule is OPTIMAL. CALM is grounded; HC-optimality is the bet.
- Open-world / lattice-growth-during-computation (Parallel-Decomposition §8.2 Q8) is an unresolved reach question directly relevant to a COMPILER: the lattice grows as new types/constraints accumulate during elaboration. 'How does the parallel-decomposition claim survive? Is there a Hasse-evolution discipline?' For a super-optimizing compiler whose network grows monotonically (every fresh-meta adds a cell — Categorical Foundations §2.3 mode-dependent dynamics), the Hasse adjacency is a MOVING target; the optimality guarantee for a fixed lattice may not transfer cleanly to an evolving one.

**Novelty / prior art:**
- HONEST POSTURE — the doc's own thesis is explicitly COMBINATORIAL novelty, not first-of-anything: 'The combination is novel; whether the combination is also faster, more correct, or more usable is an empirical question' (§6 speculative). It does NOT claim to beat LLVM, egg, or HVM on their own turf; it claims the 12-dimension combination on one substrate is unheld by any current production compiler (§4 table).
- PRIOR ART for the network-IS-IR / phase-collapse move: MLIR's multi-level-IR-with-dialects is the acknowledged closest analog and the doc explicitly frames itself as 'the same move MLIR made but at runtime granularity' (§3.1). The DIFFERENTIATOR is static-pass vs runtime granularity + dialect-free-at-runtime (lattice domains ARE dialects). The eqsat MLIR dialect (arXiv:2505.09363) and DialEgg (CGO 2025) are the closest e-graph-as-dialect prior art, but the doc positions them as 'bolted on (separate dialect, separate engine)' — the absence of a separate engine is the novel part.
- PRIOR ART for cost-intrinsic-in-cells: egg's saturate-then-extract-by-cost (Willsey 2021), TVM/Halide external cost models. NOVEL FRAMING: cost as the MERGE operation rather than a post-saturation extraction (§3.2). Tropical-semiring Viterbi-cheapest-derivation is classical; putting it as a cell-lattice merge in a propagator-network compiler is the Prologos-specific synthesis (Module Theory §6).
- PRIOR ART for the optimality (Hasse) claim: Garg's Lattice-Linear-Predicate framework (SPAA 2020+) is the STRONGEST single precedent (Parallel-Decomposition §7.4) — for finite distributive lattices with lattice-linear predicates, the Hasse poset IS the parallel decomposition (yields parallel Gale-Shapley, Dijkstra, MST, knapsack). What Prologos adds beyond Garg: universal scope (vs distributive-only), variety-graded toolkit (vs one tool), substrate (vs per-problem algorithms), self-hosting closure. The universal extension of Garg is the explicit external-review falsifiability target.
- PRIOR ART for adhesive correctness: e-graphs-are-adhesive is an EXTERNAL result (Biondo, Castelnovo, Gadducci, CALCO 2025) the doc cites for free-correctness; combined with prop:ctor-desc-tag no-critical-pairs (internal, SRE Track 2 PIR) it gives confluence-by-construction. The claim 'we don't trade speed for correctness; adhesive theory says the optimization is correct AND parallelizable; Cranelift's acyclic compromise is unnecessary for our substrate' (§3.3) is a genuine architectural differentiator vs Cranelift's production ægraphs.
- APPARENT NOVELTY (per Parallel-Decomposition §7.3, externally-flagged): (1) Hyperlattice Conjecture universal form; (2) the algorithmic↔information-flow duality specific framing (Kowalski 'Algorithm = Logic + Control' is closest analog but says different things); (3) 'entire compiler IS a propagator network at production-scale dependent types' (Lean4/Idris2/Coq/Agda have propagator-ADJACENT infrastructure but none claims propagator-network-UNIFORM architecture); (4) variety-relative-free-lattice canonicalization at runtime as a compiler concern.
- PRIOR ART for the fixpoint-engine framing: Tarski 1955 + abstract-interpretation (Cousot-Cousot 1977) for universality (well-established, INHERITED); Kahn 1974 process networks (monotone-continuous-function least-fixpoint) as the semantic antecedent of propagator networks; CALM (Hellerstein 2010) for monotone⇒coordination-free. The doc INHERITS these and is honest that the universality half of the conjecture is well-established — only the optimality half is staked.
- Categorical-foundations prior art: polynomial functors (Spivak-Niu 2024 arXiv:2312.00990) as the typing foundation; Lafont interaction nets (1990); Girard Geometry of Interaction (the GoI execution formula EX(σ) = (1-σπ)⁻¹·σ·(1-π) reframed as a fixpoint = run-to-quiescence, Categorical Foundations §10.3). The Muroya-Ghica Dynamic GoI Machine (DGoIM, combines token-passing + graph-rewriting) is named (§2.5/§7 Q5) as a candidate FORMAL execution model for .pnet — 'Could .pnet execution literally be a DGoIM, parameterized by lattice?' — an open novel theoretical direction.

**Open questions:**
- GROUNDING GAP (R-lens / Network Reality Check): The phase-collapse thesis asserts typing/reduction/optimization/codegen rules 'all fire on the same network as propagators' (§3.1), but the doc is Stage 0/1 and does not demonstrate that the CURRENT elaborator's typing rules are genuine net-add-propagator firings vs imperative infer dispatched through registries. The workflow.md Network Reality Check rule exists precisely because PPN Track 4 risked this divergence. UNCONFIRMED here (out of read scope): how much of the typing phase is ACTUALLY on-network at HEAD vs function-call-shaped. Flagged for a code-grounding facet, not resolvable from these research docs.
- The doc claims codegen rules fire on the network (§3.1) but provides NO concrete codegen-propagator design — codegen-collapse is the most speculative leg and has no grounding tier entry in §6 at all (it is neither 'grounded,' 'designed,' nor explicitly 'speculative' — it is simply asserted in the thesis sentence). Open: is there ANY design for emission-as-propagators, or is 'codegen rules fire on the network' purely aspirational? (SH master Track 2 'Low-PNet IR' is the nearest placeholder; ⬜ no code.)
- Does interaction-net order-independence / beta-optimality (HVM2's parallelism dividend) ACTUALLY carry to propagator networks? §6 flags this SPECULATIVE ('propagator networks are MORE GENERAL than interaction nets... might or might not carry; need to verify'). This is load-bearing for the parallelism half of the super-optimization claim and is unverified. Categorical Foundations §10.2 asserts interaction nets ARE polynomial functors and interaction rules ARE natural transformations — but that is a structural correspondence, not a proof that order-independence transfers.
- Is CALM the same theorem as the Hyperlattice optimality claim, or do they layer? (Parallel-Decomposition §8.1 Q1.) The super-optimization thesis needs the LAYERING reading (CALM = correctness; HC = optimality) for 'provably optimal' to mean more than 'provably correct.' Unresolved in the docs.
- What is the right cost lattice for PReduce extraction (§7 Q1)? Tropical min-plus vs richer combined quantales (instruction-count × code-size × power × latency). The algebra is given; the combination is an undecided design choice that gates the optimization-collapse story.
- How does dependent typing interact with equality saturation (§7 Q2, Moss 2025 'E-Graphs with Bindings')? Binders complicate rewriting; both Categorical Foundations §9.1 (dependent polynomial functors / lift Poly to Set/U) and PReduce's e-graph face the same binding-structure problem. Open frontier, not engineering.
- Does .pnet content-addressing scale (§6 speculative)? CHAMP scales; whether the FULL propagator-network value hashes cleanly at scale is empirical and unproven. The 'fixpoint-equal with same derivation tree' equivalence (§3.6) is stronger than IPVM but untested at large-program scale.
- UNVERIFIED quantitative grounding claims (not checked against code in this read, cited from §6): atms.rkt 877 lines, qtt.rkt 2462 lines, capability-inference.rkt 655 lines, session-propagators.rkt 633 lines, .pnet ~735ms-vs-~20s. These are stated as 'grounded today' but I did not open the .rkt files to confirm line counts or timings. Flag-don't-guess: treat as INFERRED-from-doc, not independently VERIFIED.
- Self-hosting closure recursion (Parallel-Decomposition §8.3 Q11): if the compiler IS a propagator network parameterized by lattice variety, what variety is the compiler's OWN metadata in? Recursive-metadata structure is named but unresolved — relevant because the super-optimization thesis's most novel claim (#4 in §7.3) is exactly this self-hosting closure.
- Is the adhesive guarantee LOAD-BEARING for super-optimization at scale (§7 Q6)? Cranelift gives up some correctness for production speed; adhesive theory says Prologos need not — but whether the correctness guarantees translate into MEASURABLE perf benefits when combined with the cost lattice is an open empirical question, not yet testable (gated on PReduce).


## IG4 — .pnet as network-as-value → lowered IR: the real design, current limitations, and the specialized-cell-declaration → LLVM-directive bridge (NOT the contributor PoC)

**Mode:** internal-doc  |  **SHA:** e5ede4b2bfffd0f672b7b574b451667112ad522f (matches expected)

**Sources:**
- (our-doc) docs/tracking/2026-04-30_SH_MASTER.md — SH series master tracker. Track 1 (.pnet network-as-value), Track 2 (Low-PNet IR: 'cells as typed memory regions, propagators as functions, scheduler as worklist data structure'), open-Q1 (merge-fns-as-closures → symbolic refs / NTT-style propagators-as-data), open-Q2 (IR thickness gated on PReduce). Two-axis retirement framing.
- (our-doc) docs/research/2026-04-30_SELF_HOSTING_PATH_AND_BOOTSTRAP.md — Stage 0/1 research note. §4 '.pnet as the structural linchpin' — the precise gap statement: today .pnet captures elaboration state but REPLACES live networks with sentinels and rebuilds fresh; merge functions are Racket closures that must become SYMBOLIC references in the SRE form registry, resolved at load, connecting to NTT propagators-as-data.
- (our-doc) racket/prologos/pnet-serialize.rkt — Current .pnet implementation on main (PNET_VERSION 1, 679 LoC). VERIFIED what it captures vs drops: serializes env-snapshot + 17 registries (indices 2-23) via struct→vector + gensym tagging + foreign-proc dynamic-require relinking; line 104/428 replace prop-network with '(runtime-prop-network) sentinel and rebuild via make-prop-network on load; line 99/440 procedures (merge-fns, expanders, marshallers) become 'foreign-proc stubs.
- (our-doc) docs/tracking/2026-05-20_PM_TRACK13_IMPLEMENTATION_NOTE.md — PM Track 13 Stage-0 SEED note. The compiler-technology framing: Cells=IR storage primitives, Propagators=IR computation primitives, Scheduler=IR interpreter, scheduler-state (stratum-handler registry, worklist, fuel) = network-resident IR. §5.1 Q4/Q5: handler-as-procedure (Option α, complicates .pnet — closures don't serialize) vs handler-as-data (Option β, serializable, aligns with SH/NTT/LLVM lowering).
- (our-doc) docs/tracking/standups/2026-05-17_dailies.md (lines 29-74, 66 esp.) — The Q2b CORE source. §4.6 specialized cell type framework = IR vocabulary insight (line 66): ':tier hot tells LLVM to emit fast-path code; :storage monotone-counter tells LLVM to emit atomic fixnum operations; :fires-on threshold-crossing tells LLVM to elide per-write notification.' Line 68/4735: Zip/Zig+LLVM PoC proved .pnet→LLVM lowering feasible (weeks-to-months); LHC (Logos Hyperlattice Compiler) framing — network IS compiler, .pnet IS the IR, eventually no sexp-IR.
- (our-doc) racket/prologos/specialized-cells.rkt + propagator.rkt:327-328, 1350-1388 — VERIFIED the concrete IR-vocabulary carrier in code. struct specialized-cell-meta (tier storage fires-on on-write-check on-read-check merge-fn) at propagator.rkt:327; registered per-cell via net-register-specialized-cell (:tier/:storage/:fires-on/:on-write-check/:on-read-check/:contradicts?/:domain keywords) at 1350. specialized-cells.rkt convenience ctors: make-monotone-counter-meta ('hot 'monotone-counter 'threshold-crossing), make-cold-general-meta ('cold 'general 'any-change), make-warm-general-meta ('warm 'general 'any-change).
- (our-doc) racket/prologos/propagator.rkt:1077-1193 — VERIFIED the production specialized-cell IR vocabulary in use: 8 net-register-specialized-cell call sites already declaring well-known cells — fuel-cost (hot/monotone-counter/threshold-crossing), fuel-budget (cold), retraction-stratum (warm/seteq), resolution-stratum (warm/list), fork-on-union, fork-contradiction, decomposed-positions, contradicted-branch-aids, union-derivation-chains. Each carries an explicit merge-fn + tier/storage/fires-on declaration — the directive set a backend would interpret.

**Key findings:**
- TWO SEPARABLE TRANSFORMS. The human's Q2a/Q2b are two distinct extensions of .pnet, gated differently. Q2a = round-trip LIVE NETWORK STRUCTURE (topology + merges + propagator bodies) so .pnet becomes a deployment artifact (Track 1, gates Stage A.5). Q2b = lower the per-cell SPECIALIZED-CELL DECLARATIONS to LLVM directives so a native backend interprets them (the §4.6-framework-as-IR-vocabulary insight, already partly realized in code today). Q2b is the more mature of the two — the declaration vocabulary EXISTS in production (specialized-cell-meta struct, 8 live call sites); Q2a is the unbuilt linchpin.
- WHAT .pnet CAPTURES TODAY (VERIFIED, pnet-serialize.rkt). A PNET_VERSION-1 file is a 24-slot list: version, source-hash, serialized env-snapshot, specs, definition-locations, exports, namespace, then 17 registries (preparse, ctor, type-meta, multi-defn, subtype, coercion, capability, trait, impl, param-impl, specialization, tycon-arity, bundle, defn-params, trait-laws, property, functor). Serialization = struct→vector recursion + gensym tagging (symbol$$N) + foreign-proc relinking via dynamic-require on (source-module, racket-name). This is ELABORATION STATE — 'what was elaborated', the cell VALUES and registry contributions.
- WHAT .pnet DROPS TODAY — THE GAP (VERIFIED, pnet-serialize.rkt:104,428,99,440). THREE things are NOT round-tripped: (1) the live prop-network and elab-network are replaced with sentinels '(runtime-prop-network)/'(runtime-elab-network) on serialize and rebuilt via make-prop-network FRESH on deserialize (lines 104/428-432) — so the TOPOLOGY of what-watches-what is lost, not serialized; (2) any procedure value (merge functions, preparse expanders, non-foreign marshallers) becomes a 'foreign-proc stub that errors if invoked (lines 99/440) — merge functions as Racket CLOSURES are the central blocker; (3) propagator BODIES (fire-fns) are Racket closures, equally unserializable. The doc's own framing: '.pnet captures what was elaborated, not the live network that did the elaborating.'
- Q2a TRANSFORM DESIGN (what .pnet must BECOME). To round-trip live structure, .pnet must add three serializable sub-formats beyond the 24-slot value/registry payload: (A) a CELL TABLE — each cell as {id, initial-value, domain-name, specialized-cell-meta declaration}; the meta is already a #:transparent struct of plain symbols/predicates so its DECLARATIVE fields serialize trivially (the only non-serializable field is merge-fn-as-closure, addressed by B). (B) a MERGE/PROPAGATOR-BODY symbol table — replace each Racket closure with a SYMBOLIC reference into the SRE form registry (per §4 of the path note), resolved at load to the registered merge/fire implementation; this is the 'merge-functions-are-Racket-closures problem' solution. (C) a TOPOLOGY EDGE LIST — each propagator as {input-cids, output-cids, body-symbol-ref, broadcast-profile, flags, component-paths, assumption/decision-cell tags} — i.e. a serialization of the propagator struct (propagator.rkt:362) with the fire-fn replaced by a symbolic body-ref. The deserializer then reconstructs topology by re-installing propagators from the edge list rather than rebuilding 'fresh and empty'.
- Q2b TRANSFORM DESIGN — the specialized-cell-declaration → LLVM-directive bridge (the human's CORE ask). VERIFIED the carrier: specialized-cell-meta = (tier storage fires-on on-write-check on-read-check merge-fn) at propagator.rkt:327. The dailies line 66 gives the exact directive mapping a backend interprets: :tier 'hot → emit fast-path / hot-region code; :storage 'monotone-counter → emit atomic fixnum ops (lock-free increment, no CHAMP); :fires-on 'threshold-crossing → elide per-write dependent-notification (only notify on on-write-check firing). Extending the family: :storage 'e-class → union-find-keyed memory (PReduce); :durability 'persistent → CHAMP/.pnet-cache-backed; :concurrency 'lock-free / :distribution 'crdt → scheduler-variant directives (dailies line 74: 'each runtime READS THE SAME DECLARATIONS DIFFERENTLY'). Critically: these are ALREADY plain-symbol struct fields, so they serialize as-is into the Q2a cell table — no closure problem for the DECLARATION half. Only the merge-fn field needs the Q2a symbol-ref treatment.
- THE LOWERING IS THE SAME ARTIFACT AT THREE INTERPRETATION LAYERS. Per PM-13 §2 + dailies: a .pnet that round-trips structure is simultaneously (i) loadable by the Racket scheduler (interpreter), (ii) lowerable to LLVM IR where cells=typed memory regions, propagators=functions over those regions, scheduler=worklist data structure (SH Track 2 Low-PNet IR), and (iii) the LHC compiler's own IR for self-hosting (Stage B). The specialized-cell-meta declarations are the directives that make (ii) emit FAST code rather than a literal interpreter. The Racket runtime hitting 3.94-4.52 ns/cell-write under these declarations is the proof-of-concept that the SAME declarations under LLVM 'will dramatically beat this' (dailies line 66).
- PRODUCTION ALREADY SPEAKS THE IR VOCABULARY (VERIFIED, propagator.rkt:1077-1193). 8 well-known cells are registered with explicit tier/storage/fires-on/merge declarations today: fuel-cost (hot/monotone-counter/threshold-crossing — the canonical LLVM-atomic-fixnum candidate), fuel-budget (cold), retraction/resolution-stratum (warm), 4 fork/union/contradiction request accumulators. This is not aspirational: the IR vocabulary for Q2b is in the codebase and exercised. The gap is purely that pnet-serialize.rkt sentinelizes the network rather than serializing these cells' declarations + topology.
- HANDLER-AS-DATA IS A Q2a SUB-PROBLEM (PM-13 §5.1 Q4/Q5). The stratum-handler registry (stratum-handlers box, propagator.rkt) and the handlers themselves are off-network Racket procedures TODAY. PM-13 explicitly notes Option α (handler-as-procedure-in-cell) 'complicates' .pnet because closures don't serialize, while Option β (handler-as-data: transition encoded as cell+propagator structure the scheduler interprets) 'aligns with SH'. So the scheduler-state IR (worklist, fuel, worldview-cache, handler registry) is part of what Q2a must round-trip; fuel-cost and worldview-cache are ALREADY on-network cells (precedent), making them the easy cases; the handler registry is the foundational-redesign case.

**PReduce implications:**
- GATING DIRECTION IS PReduce → Q2 IR THICKNESS, not the reverse (SH Master open-Q2; path note §5). The decision of HOW MANY layers sit between propagator network and LLVM IR depends on PReduce outputs: if super-optimization (e-graph equality saturation, tropical/quantale cost extraction, DPO rewriting) happens AT THE PROPAGATOR-NETWORK LAYER, then the Low-PNet IR below it can be THINNER (less optimization machinery needed in the lowering). So PReduce delivery directly shapes the .pnet→LLVM transform's complexity.
- PReduce contributes NEW IR vocabulary that Q2b's lowering must interpret (dailies line 72). ':storage 'e-class (union-find-keyed cells), :fires-on 'min-cost-decrease, :durability 'persistent' are framework extensions that become directives: a backend lowering :storage 'e-class emits union-find-backed memory; :durability 'persistent emits CHAMP/.pnet-cache-backed storage. PReduce is the first consumer of the multi-dim tropical quantale (§4.2) — its cost dimensions tensored via Galois bridges must also serialize into the Q2a topology (bridges = cross-domain propagators with symbolic body-refs).
- ORDERING: PReduce-first, then SH (path note §5). Land PReduce's ALGORITHMIC reduction win in Racket (reduction is 64% of compile time per Track 3 perf data); THEN the .pnet→LLVM lowering ports an already-fast algorithm and the constant-factor gain compounds multiplicatively. Q2a/Q2b work has independent value (path note: Track 1 .pnet extension can proceed in parallel with PReduce since it's independent), but the SUPER-OPTIMIZING-compiler claim that makes self-hosted Prologos competitive waits on PReduce.
- PReduce's cost-bounded scheduling is a candidate stratum whose scheduler-state must be on-network for .pnet round-trip (PM-13 §6.1). PReduce e-class extraction depends on cost-bounded scheduling; PM-13's scheduler-state-cell pattern (fuel-cost cell as precedent) is the template. For Q2a to round-trip a PReduce-enabled network, the cost-extraction work-queue must be a declared specialized cell (e.g., promote to monotone-counter or direct-ref cache per specialized-cells.rkt forward-compat note), not an off-network worklist.

**.pnet/LLVM implications:**
- THE TRANSFORM, STATED PRECISELY (Q2a). Current .pnet = serialize(elaboration-state) with network→sentinel. Target .pnet = serialize(elaboration-state) ⊕ serialize(CELL-TABLE) ⊕ serialize(MERGE/BODY-SYMBOL-TABLE) ⊕ serialize(TOPOLOGY-EDGE-LIST). The three new sub-formats: CELL-TABLE entries are {cell-id, initial-value, domain-name, (tier storage fires-on on-write-check on-read-check)} — note the specialized-cell-meta is already #:transparent and all-symbols EXCEPT merge-fn, so it serializes by the existing struct→vector path unchanged. MERGE/BODY-SYMBOL-TABLE replaces every Racket closure (merge-fn, fire-fn, on-write-check, on-read-check, expander, marshaller) with a symbolic registry key resolved at load. TOPOLOGY-EDGE-LIST serializes the propagator struct (inputs outputs body-ref broadcast-profile flags srcloc) + dependent-entry (paths assumption-id decision-cell-id) per edge.
- THE CLOSURE PROBLEM IS THE LINCHPIN BLOCKER (path note §4, pnet-serialize.rkt:99,104,440). Today: (a) merge functions are Racket closures → 'foreign-proc stub on serialize; (b) the whole network is replaced with make-prop-network on load. The named solution (path note §4): merge functions become SYMBOLIC references registered in the SRE FORM REGISTRY, dispatched at load time — i.e. propagators-as-DATA not propagators-as-Racket-procedures (NTT-style declarative definitions).
