A native backend reading a CELL-TABLE entry emits: :tier 'hot → hot memory region + inlined fast-path read/write; :storage 'monotone-counter → ATOMIC FIXNUM cell (lock-free increment, bypass CHAMP merge) — the canonical example is the fuel-cost cell; :storage 'general → CHAMP/hashmap-backed; :fires-on 'threshold-crossing → emit dependent-notification ONLY inside the on-write-check-true branch (elide notification on the common monotone-grow case); :fires-on 'any-change → notify on every write; on-write-check (symbolic-ref-resolved) → inline predicate that writes the contradiction cell structurally when true. PReduce extensions: :storage 'e-class → union-find-keyed memory; :durability 'persistent → CHAMP/.pnet-cache-backed; :fires-on 'min-cost-decrease. The Racket runtime hitting 3.94-4.52 ns/cell-write under these declarations is the PoC that the SAME declarations under LLVM 'will dramatically beat this.' Every runtime READS THE SAME DECLARATIONS DIFFERENTLY (Racket scheduler / LLVM / WASM / future CRDT).

PART 3 — AVOIDING THE CALL-SHIM / INTERPRETER-WIREUP TRAP (the deepest Q2b risk, FR3/FR2): the runtime-on-cells model is STRUCTURALLY PRONE to lowering cell reads/writes + propagator firing as a SEQUENCE OF OPAQUE RUNTIME-LIBRARY CALLS — LLVM cannot see across an opaque call (no inline, no alias proof, no redundant-load elim, no SROA, no vectorize). CHAMP/hasheq cell stores are pointer-rich = the OPPOSITE of coalesced/SSA-promotable layout (the pattern that gave V8 7× L1 dcache misses → V8 ABANDONED sea-of-nodes in 2025). MITIGATIONS, none designed today, all required: (a) a flat columnar/SoA mirror of cell state for the backend (NOT the CHAMP, which is for CPU speculation/structure-sharing); (b) pure-SSA emission for single-write propagators (checklist [1]); (c) the custom dispatch cc ([6]); (d) confine opaque calls to the effect/FFI stratum ([3]/Part-3); (e) TBAA roots from SRE domains ([4]); (f) precise-RC over statepoints ([9]). VERIFICATION GATE (the 'Network Reality Check' analog for the LLVM boundary): after emission, assert via `opt` that hot kernels are (i) free of opaque calls, (ii) carry the declared memory()/noalias attributes (LLVM's own attribute inference confirms/denies), (iii) survive mem2reg with zero residual entry-block allocas for pure-SSA propagators. KEY DISTINCTION: 'the network IS the IR' is true for the OPTIMIZING IR; it is a CATEGORY ERROR for the EXECUTION representation — the optimizing network and the cache-local lowered form are different objects.


## phase_problem_collapse

Q2c — the mechanism by which a multi-pass compiler collapses to a lattice fixpoint on the propagator network, positioned HONESTLY against the canon.

THE MECHANISM (super-optimizing-compiler §3.1, VERBATIM): 'the propagator network is itself the intermediate representation. There is no high-level IR + optimization framework on top stack; there is one substrate, with cells holding lattice values and propagators implementing the lattice merges. Typing rules, reduction rules, optimization rules, and code-generation rules all fire on the same network as propagators.' The MLIR-at-runtime-granularity analogy (§3.1): dialects = SRE-registered domains; passes = propagator firings; lowering = a stratified scheduler choosing which propagators fire when. Phase ORDERING is dissolved (§3.7): the perennial 'is pass A before or after B?' is REPLACED by 'what stratum does this rule live in?', and the stratum is forced by the rule's ALGEBRAIC properties (monotone → S0; non-monotone → higher stratum; topology-changing → topology stratum) — ordering EMERGES from algebra, not pipeline configuration. The collapse is coherent because the compiler is ONE monotone-fixpoint computation (run-to-quiescence); Module-Theory §12 gives the precise form ('a fixpoint of an endomorphism on a module over a lattice-ordered ring'). The utm-fl audit's precise H2 statement: phase boundaries 'reduce to STRATUM BOUNDARIES on the same network, not separations between distinct artifacts.' The categorical witness C4.f (structural conjecture, load-bearing): the SAME Kleene-star/trace operator fires on typing cells at compile-time and reduction cells at run-time — 'the algorithm is invariant; only the cells differ' (run-to-quiescence IS the GoI machine: geometric series = Kleene star in a traced semilattice-enriched SMC).

HONEST POSITIONING AGAINST THE CANON (FR2/FR4 — the headline is ~85% well-trodden, ~15% genuinely novel, and the novel 15% is NOT 'it's a fixpoint'):
- Kildall 1973 + Cousot-Cousot 1977 + Tarski 1955: 'optimization/analysis = least fixpoint of a monotone function on a lattice' is FOUNDING, not novel.
- SCCP (Wegman-Zadeck 1985, shipping in LLVM/GCC for 40 years): analysis+optimization FUSED in ONE lattice fixpoint, provably stronger than separate constant-prop + DCE iterated in any order. The precision argument Prologos wants to make IS the SCCP argument — and it is the strongest available framing because it is grounded in a 40-year-old shipping result. CAVEAT: SCCP fuses two ANALYSES; the rewrite is a post-fixpoint pass.
- Lerner-Grove-Chambers POPL 2002 'Composing Dataflow Analyses and Transformations' = the DIRECT ANCESTOR: independently-specified analyses+transformations communicate IMPLICITLY (via graph transformations) to a mutual optimistic 'super-analysis' fixpoint. Prologos's cells+propagators+BSP-fixpoint IS this, generalized from a fixed handful of dataflow analyses to ALL stages.
- Bautista-Jensen-Monat-Miné PLDI 2024 'Compiling with Abstract Interpretation': the COMPILER ITSELF as abstract interpretation; functor domains = passes; SSA DERIVED as an abstract domain. Directly anticipates 'compiler-as-fixpoint.'
- Equality saturation (Tate et al. 2009; egg 2021): non-destructive rewriting + separate cost extraction = the heart of PReduce. egglog PLDI 2025 + 'Optimism in Equality Saturation' PLDI 2026 already unify Datalog+eqsat+abstract-interpretation into ONE fixpoint with optimistic (TOP-start, à la SCCP) e-class analysis — the analysis+rewriting HALF of PReduce, peer-reviewed and shipping, on the SAME e-graph+Datalog+AI triad.
- Sea-of-nodes (Click ~1995): value-based IR dissolves scheduling — AND V8 ABANDONED it in 2025 (compile time halved, 7× fewer L1 misses). A live counter-datapoint the docs must metabolize, not ignore.

THE DEFENSIBLE CLAIM (precisely scoped): the MONOTONE inter-pass phase ordering collapses into ONE S0 least-fixpoint over a product of SRE-typed lattice domains (Lerner-Grove-Chambers super-analysis lifted to SUBSTRATE scope). The OVER-claim to avoid: 'phase ordering is eliminated.' The genuinely-hard residue — non-monotone/destructive choices + NP-hard cost extraction — RELOCATES to (a) the strata above S0 and (b) tropical extraction, not vanishes. The stratum stack IS itself a 'phase order,' defensible ONLY because strata are ordered by a FORCED fixpoint-requirement partial-order derived from each rule's algebra (Sk fires only after S0..S(k-1) quiesce), not chosen for performance — but the docs ASSERT this derivation; they must EXHIBIT it (given a rule, which stratum, why uniquely). 'Collapse the front, phase-separate the back' (FR1): collapse typing/elaboration/reduction BUT keep a phase boundary before codegen extraction+lowering — Cranelift deliberately does NOT fold lowering into the rewrite fixpoint because lowering extraction needs a SETTLED cost surface (can't pick instructions while the e-graph still grows). This contradicts the maximalist 'codegen rules fire on the same network' framing (the least-grounded leg, no concrete codegen-propagator design).

NOT-YET-GROUNDED (the gap the main session must close): NO facet confirms the CURRENT elaborator typing is actually net-add-propagator/net-cell-write at HEAD vs function-call-shaped dispatched through registries. The Network Reality Check rule EXISTS because PPN Track 4 'built function-call wrappers around the same imperative infer' after three critique rounds validated a propagator-native architecture. So present-tense 'the compiler IS a propagator network' may be aspirational for typing too, not just reduction. The PARTIAL claim (everything-except-evaluation, with E1: type-inference↔constraint-resolution already dissolved within S0) is rigorous and shipped; the FULL claim (including reduction) is aspirational pending PReduce.


## non_von_neumann_reach

- LOCK IN NOW (cheap, load-bearing): MAKE ACTIVATION SETS EXPLICIT on-network. Gunrock's frontier (arXiv:1701.01170) + GDlog's semi-naive delta (arXiv:2311.02206) prove the winning sparse-parallel-iteration pattern is an explicit active subset processed per BSP round. Treat 'which cells/propagators are live this round' as a first-class on-network object (a frontier/delta cell), NOT an implicit consequence of worklist state. This IS the difference between a GPU/dataflow-reachable .pnet and a Racket-only one — MLIR/Triton/XLA will never recover the parallelism from imperative scheduler code (the gpu dialect 'requires explicitly parallel IR and doesn't do GPU parallelization').
- LOCK IN NOW: KEEP EVERY CELL MERGE ASSOCIATIVE + COMMUTATIVE (+ idempotent where possible). This single property unlocks segmented-reduction / gpu.all_reduce / gpu.subgroup_reduce (GPU), associative content-addressable hardware, AND dataflow-chip reduction units. Prologos's CALM-monotone join-semilattices already satisfy it; PROTECT it — treat any proposed non-assoc/non-comm merge (including a non-associative cost tie-break) as forfeiting ALL parallel-hardware targets, requiring explicit justification. Serialize the assoc/comm/idem properties as first-class .pnet cell metadata (Prologos already declares them via register-merge-fn!/SRE) so a backend picks segmented-reduction vs serial-fold per cell.
- LOCK IN NOW: AVOID POINTER-CHASING CELL STORES on the backend path. CHAMP/hasheq persistent maps are great for CPU speculation/structure-sharing but GPU-HOSTILE (GDlog's HISA dense columnar store beats pointer/hash stores; the 35-45× speedup hinges on coalesced contiguous access — same pattern that gave V8 7× L1 misses). Introduce a flat COLUMNAR / struct-of-arrays mirror of cell state (cell-id-indexed columns per SRE domain) as the backend-facing representation, decided NOW even if populated lazily, so the persistent-map design doesn't foreclose it.
- LOCK IN NOW: BUCKET PROPAGATORS BY KIND so each fire-fn-kind is a uniform divergence-free kernel. SIMT/dataflow fabrics punish heterogeneous control flow; the broadcast-propagator (one propagator, N items, one fire; measured 75.6× at N=100) is already this pattern. A BSP round should lower to one kernel PER propagator-kind over its activation set, not one kernel over a heterogeneous mixed worklist. Record propagator-kind tags in .pnet.
- DESIGN THE POST-EXTRACTION HANDOFF AS TARGET-NEUTRAL (the architecture fork that keeps non-vN reachable). The docs commit to .pnet-DIRECT-to-LLVM (preserve single-fixpoint, no MLIR); but FR3/FR5 show an intermediate regionalized-dataflow representation (RVSDG-like, TACO 2020 — 'control flow as data flow ... enables easy conversion to parallel circuits') better preserves the discovered parallelism to GPU/CGRA, and is the production-validated 'optimize high, lower late, pick backend last' shape. RECOMMEND: emit a target-neutral dataflow DAG from extraction; make LLVM-emit and GPU-emit SIBLING consumers. Direct-and-only-to-LLVM re-sequentializes the all-in-parallel structure and couples the whole compiler to the von-Neumann target.
- BOUND DYNAMIC TOPOLOGY TO STRATUM BOUNDARIES + pre-reserve capacity. Cells/propagators allocated mid-fixpoint (topology stratum) are hostile to ANY static fabric (GPUs hate reallocation — GDlog uses Eager Buffer Management full+k*delta to amortize; CGRAs are statically placed). The CALM-safe protocol (topology change only at stratum boundaries, per stratification.md) + EBM-style pre-reservation gives a FIXED-per-round graph — the contract that keeps both GPU and CGRA backends viable. This may bifurcate the roadmap: GPU for dynamic-topology networks, CGRA only for topology-frozen ones.
- ENRICHMENT ANNOTATION + ADDRESS-SPACE HINTS in .pnet (the lowering layer reads them to pick the parallelism strategy): per-cell :semilattice (S0) / :commutative-monoid (S(-1)) / :Q-module+quantale-id (cost) determines IN-fragment → HVM2-style massive parallelism vs adhesive-DPO → DPOI critical-pair-aware scheduling (engineering-memo §2 Track-0.3). Hot per-round activation/delta cells → GPU global (coalesced); within-round scratch → workgroup; constant registries (trait tables, ctor-desc) → constant memory. Prologos's hot/warm/cold storage-strategy hooks are the right place to record an address-space HINT per cell NOW so it isn't reverse-engineered later.
- FAR-HORIZON SYNERGY worth preserving cheaply: PReduce's OWN e-graph could double as the spatial-mapping engine for a CGRA/dataflow accelerator — FlexC (CGRA survey) maps kernels via equality saturation, expanding coverage 2.2×. The same saturation that super-optimizes the program discovers how to lay it out on the fabric. A dataflow CHIP (NextSilicon Maverick-2, Efficient Computer Electron E1) is a MORE faithful target than a GPU (no SIMT impedance mismatch — cells→PEs, propagators→routing, merges→in-fabric reduction). Keep .pnet a clean spatial dataflow graph (cells, edges, merge-algebra, activation) and BOTH GPU and dataflow-chip backends stay in reach. HONEST CAVEAT: every non-vN claim in our corpus is INFERRED (the term barely appears); and Cranelift's avg-1.13-e-nodes finding means the COMPILER'S OWN networks may never be wide enough to benefit — GPU/dataflow is plausibly only for USER programs compiled to big BSP-LE/e-graph/relational fixpoints, not for the compiler itself. Measure network width before betting on it.


## frontier_synthesis

PRODUCTION FRONTIER (decision-altering): exactly ONE compiler ships an e-graph as a real codegen-adjacent mid-end — Cranelift's aegraph (Fallin, Apr-2026 retrospective, https://cfallin.org/blog/2026/04/09/aegraph/). It is EAGER single-pass cost-reducing rewriting, NOT saturation (acyclic by construction). Non-memorizable production metrics: avg e-class = 1.13 e-nodes (~88% singletons), 2 suboptimal extractions in 4 MILLION value-nodes, 7-8% MORE compile time for ~2% runtime win, and the fat-e-class machinery 'may not be pulling its weight' — most of the win is structural-sharing + good code-placement (the cheap part). VALIDATED dual-use: the SAME ISLE DSL drives both mid-end rewrites AND instruction lowering (RFC: https://github.com/bytecodealliance/rfcs/blob/main/accepted/cranelift-egraph.md) — but lowering runs as a SEPARATE phase over the e-graph output (one registry, two phases). This directly challenges PReduce's saturate-to-fixpoint default.

EXTRACTION is NP-COMPLETE for the realistic DAG/sharing-aware cost, settled rigorously: reduction from min-set-cover (https://effect.systems/blog/egraph-extraction.html), and inapprox-to-ANY-constant-factor via an e-graph↔monotone-Boolean-circuit bijection (E-Graphs as Circuits / Optimal Extraction via Treewidth, https://arxiv.org/html/2408.17042 — FPT-in-treewidth 2^O(w²)·poly when the e-graph is thin). TRACTABILITY LEVERS our docs miss: sparse-graph optimal extraction (OOPSLA 2024, https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3689801) and e-boost warm-start-ILP (extraction = 89.3% of eqsat runtime; 558× vs vanilla ILP at +5.6% quality, https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.13020). The per-TARGET cost gap is REAL & unsolved algebraically: real 7nm area/delay is NON-linear & NON-monotone, so E-Syn LEARNS it with XGBoost (R²≈0.77) and runs separate per-objective Pareto passes (https://arxiv.org/html/2403.14242); SmoothE (differentiable extraction, ASPLOS 2025, https://www.csl.cornell.edu/~yc2632/data/smoothe_asplos2025_final.pdf) is limited to additive/local/scalar cost. Multi-dim tropical EXISTS as prior art but as a SEMIRING not a quantale and not for codegen (Ternary Tropical Gamma Semiring, Nov-2025, https://arxiv.org/pdf/2511.18099). Instruction-selection by per-ISA-cost rule synthesis: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2405.06127.

PHASE-ORDERING DISSOLUTION CANON: combine-on-one-structure (Click-Cooper 1995, https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/201059.201061; the direct-ancestor Lerner-Grove-Chambers POPL 2002, https://cseweb.ucsd.edu/~lerner/UW-CSE-01-11-01.pdf); equality saturation (Tate et al. 2009, https://cseweb.ucsd.edu/~lerner/papers/lmcs11-eqsat.pdf; egg, https://arxiv.org/abs/2004.03082); value-based-IR — AND V8 ABANDONED sea-of-nodes in 2025 (compile time halved, 7× fewer L1 dcache misses, https://v8.dev/blog/leaving-the-sea-of-nodes). 'Solving the Phase-Ordering Problem != Globally Optimal Code' (2024, https://arxiv.org/html/2410.03120v2). COMPILER-IS-ABSTRACT-INTERPRETATION: Cousot-Cousot 1977 (https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/512950.512973) + POPL 2002 transformation-AS-AI (https://cs.nyu.edu/~pcousot/COUSOTpapers/POPL02.shtml) + 'Compiling with Abstract Interpretation' PLDI 2024 (https://hal.science/hal-04535159, SSA derived as an abstract domain). Lattice-semantics languages: Flix (https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/2908080.2908096) + Datafun (https://www.cl.cam.ac.uk/~nk480/datafun.pdf). SCCP: https://www.cs.utexas.edu/~pingali/CS380C/2010/papers/p291-wegman.pdf. THE nearest converging peer: 'Optimism in Equality Saturation' (PLDI 2026, https://arxiv.org/pdf/2511.20782) + egglog 'Better Together' (PLDI 2025, https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3591239) unify Datalog+eqsat+abstract-interpretation into ONE fixpoint with optimistic TOP-start e-class analysis.

OPTIMAL-LLVM-EMISSION canon: LLVM Frontend PerformanceTips (https://llvm.org/docs/Frontend/PerformanceTips.html); functional-front-end playbook musttail+JWA+naked (Farvardin-Reppy, https://ar5iv.labs.arxiv.org/html/1805.08842); GHC ghccc+TBAA (https://blog.llvm.org/2010/05/glasgow-haskell-compiler-and-llvm.html); precise-RC over statepoints — Lean Counting-Immutable-Beans (https://arxiv.org/pdf/1908.05647) + Koka Perceus (https://xnning.github.io/papers/perceus.pdf) + LLVM Statepoints barrier (https://llvm.org/docs/Statepoints.html); Swift OSSA 'high-level info lost on lowering' (https://github.com/swiftlang/swift/blob/main/docs/OptimizerDesign.md); Julia devirt/unbox-before-LLVM (https://janvitek.org/pubs/oopsla18b.pdf); MLIR progressive lowering 'lower too early obscures structure' (https://mlir.llvm.org/docs/Tutorials/Toy/Ch-5/). Eqsat-as-IR-dialect (closest to 'substrate IS the IR'): https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09363. Denali eqsat-to-optimal-machine-code (PLDI 2002, 24 years old): https://courses.cs.washington.edu/courses/cse501/15sp/papers/joshi.pdf. LLVM-superopt SOTA to BENCHMARK against: Minotaur SIMD (OOPSLA 2024, https://users.cs.utah.edu/~regehr/minotaur-oopsla24.pdf) + Souper (https://arxiv.org/abs/1711.04422).

NON-vN LOWERING: Datalog-on-GPU blueprint GDlog/GPUlog (35-45× over Soufflé via dense HISA + semi-naive delta + kernel-boundary BSP, https://arxiv.org/html/2311.02206v4); Gunrock frontier (https://arxiv.org/pdf/1701.01170); MLIR gpu dialect 'requires explicitly parallel IR' (https://mlir.llvm.org/docs/Dialects/GPU/); RVSDG dataflow-IR→LLVM (https://dl.acm.org/doi/fullHtml/10.1145/3391902); CGRA mapping via equality saturation / FlexC (https://www.comp.nus.edu.sg/~tulika/CGRA-Survey.pdf); live dataflow hardware (NextSilicon Maverick-2, Efficient Computer Electron E1, https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dataflow_architecture). CAVEAT to re-verify (near-cutoff): the 2026 arXiv IDs 2511.20782 (Optimism) and 2511.18099 (TTGS), and the structurally-INVALID Schlatt ID '2602.16707' in OUR docs (arXiv months are 01-12; 26 is impossible — find the real ID).


## novelty_assessment

ADOPTING THE CRITIC'S HONEST VERDICT: the headline 'the compiler collapses to a lattice fixpoint / the network IS a super-optimizing compiler' is ~85% well-trodden and ~15% genuinely novel, and the novel 15% is NOT where the docs put their rhetorical weight.

WELL-TRODDEN (each conjunct independently anticipated, most by decades): 'optimization/analysis = least fixpoint of a monotone function on a lattice' (Kildall 1973, Cousot-Cousot 1977, Tarski 1955); 'analysis+optimization fused in ONE lattice fixpoint, in production, provably stronger than sequential' (SCCP, shipping in LLVM/GCC since 1985 — the phase-collapse precision argument Prologos wants IS the SCCP argument); 'the COMPILER ITSELF as abstract interpretation, passes as functor domains, SSA derived as an abstract domain' (Bautista-Jensen-Monat-Miné PLDI 2024); 'non-destructive rewriting + separate cost extraction = equality saturation' (Tate 2009; egg 2021); 'value-based IR dissolves scheduling' (Click sea-of-nodes ~1995 — and V8 ABANDONED it in 2025, a live counter-datapoint); 'one fixpoint unifying Datalog+eqsat+abstract-interpretation with optimistic TOP-start e-class analysis' (egglog PLDI 2025 + Optimism-in-Eqsat PLDI 2026, converging on PReduce's analysis+rewriting HALF in real time on the SAME triad); 'same DSL for mid-end + lowering, one rule registry' (Cranelift ISLE, in production); 'eqsat-to-optimal-machine-code via extraction + solver' (Denali, PLDI 2002, 24 years old); 'lattice-semantics languages, fixpoints of monotone functions on user-declared lattices' (Flix, Datafun); 'tropical/semiring cost, Kleene-star = all-pairs shortest path for extraction' (Goodman 1999 + Floyd-Warshall/Bellman-Ford lineage); 'multi-dim tropical cost for joint objectives' (TTGS, Nov-2025).

GENUINELY NOVEL (the 15%, ranked by defensibility): (1) TOTALIZATION ON ONE PROPAGATOR SUBSTRATE — running the WHOLE front-to-back compiler (lex→parse→elaborate→dependent-type/QTT/session-check→reduce→codegen→logic-programming) as ONE BSP-scheduled monotone propagator network where the network IS the IR, under explicit cell/propagator/scheduler orthogonality (scheduler-portable). NO surveyed system collapses the entire compiler this way — all prior art fuses a BOUNDED set (SCCP: 2 analyses; AI-as-compiler: an analysis-derived pipeline; egglog: rewriting+analysis over terms; Lerner-Grove-Chambers: a fixed handful of dataflow analyses). This is a BREADTH/SUBSTRATE claim, not a 'new fixpoint idea' — a reviewer who knows AI-as-compilation + egglog + Flix will find every per-phase conjunct familiar. (2) SCHEDULER-PORTABILITY OF THE WHOLE COMPILER AS DEPLOYMENT THESIS — the .pnet network-as-value rehydrates and runs to the SAME fixpoint under Gauss-Seidel/BSP/Zig-LLVM/distributed (the LVars/CALM/BloomL order-independence guarantee lifted from a runtime data-structure library to an entire COMPILER); strongest evidence is the E9-bis empirical witness (a 40-line parallel scheduler 'just worked' first-try, owner-attested). (3) CROSS-SESSION .pnet PERSISTENCE — Cranelift does NOT persist (per-function, discarded); eqsat-MLIR/Schlatt persist across PHASES but not SESSIONS. Past every cited frontier; concentrate the persistence novelty here. (4) TROPICAL-QUANTALE RESIDUATION FOR COST-PROVENANCE (NOT extraction-speed) — no facet found e-graph cost extraction framed as quantale residuation (FR6: the seam is unoccupied though adjacent to active enriched-category work); but the residuation contribution is novel ONLY for backward cost-BLAME/provenance, NOT as a faster or NP-escaping extractor — the docs over-claim it as 'the extraction algorithm.' Scoped to provenance, it's a real, defensible, small contribution.

WEAKEST novelty claim: HASSE-PARALLEL-DECOMPOSITION (Hyperlattice optimality) — our own docs admit it is 'a design mandate, not a theorem' (DESIGN_PRINCIPLES.org:441); CONFIRMED only for Boolean (Hasse=hypercube, textbook), STRONG-but-prior-art for distributive (Garg LLP, SPAA 2020); the universal-across-varieties form is self-flagged APPARENT-NOVELTY-pending-falsification. The one genuinely-novel sub-claim is self-hosting closure (the compiler that compiles Hasse-driven programs is itself a Hasse-driven network — Garg has no analog), but that is a recursion claim, not a parallelism result.

VERDICT IN ONE SENTENCE: the novelty is the CONJUNCTION at full-compiler scope on a scheduler-portable propagator substrate + cross-session persistence — not any single conjunct, not the fixpoint idea, not eqsat, not the cost algebra. Sell it as 'first totalizing, scheduler-portable, dependently-typed propagator-network compiler with cross-session equality persistence'; cite SCCP/AI-as-compilation/egglog/Denali AS the lineage you EXTEND (which strengthens credibility); DROP any framing that implies 'compiler-as-fixpoint' or 'extraction-as-residuation-escapes-NP' is itself new; and position against egglog + Optimism-in-Eqsat as the NEAREST peers, not generic 'egg.' Whether the conjunction is also FASTER/BETTER than the same techniques bolted together conventionally is the genuinely-open empirical question every honest facet flags.


## open_design_questions

- TREE-COST vs DAG-COST for PReduce (the single most load-bearing decision): does the super-optimizer need DAG/sharing-aware cost (faithful to emitted-instruction sharing → NP-hard, needs e-boost-ILP/treewidth/sparse) or accept tree cost (residual, poly-time, double-counts sharing → suboptimal LLVM)? You cannot have both 'inherit the residual for free' AND 'optimal LLVM.' Whatever you pick, edit E_GRAPHS_RESEARCH §471 + TROPICAL_QUANTALE §10.7 to stop claiming the residual IS the extractor for the DAG case.
- EAGER vs SATURATE per rule-kind, and BEFORE committing the substrate: what is the actual e-class-size DISTRIBUTION on real Prologos reduction (β/δ/ι + structural + arithmetic)? Cranelift's avg-1.13-e-nodes (~88% singletons) suggests fat e-classes may be rare; if Prologos matches, Tracks 4/6 drop from central to rare-case and S0 should default to eager-cost-reducing single-pass for the deterministic rule kinds. This is the highest-value MEASUREMENT to take first.
- WHERE does sharing/register-pressure/code-motion cost live — in PReduce extraction (DAG-cost, NP-complete) or deferred to an LLVM-side placement pass (Cranelift's choice, keeps PReduce extraction tree-cheap)? 'Collapse the front, phase-separate the back' is production-validated but means PReduce does NOT do the sharing-aware optimization — dents the 'super-optimizing' scope. Decide the cost-model locus.
- DIRECT-to-LLVM vs an intermediate regionalized-dataflow IR (RVSDG-like / eqsat-MLIR dialect): the docs commit to .pnet-direct-to-LLVM (preserve single-fixpoint, no MLIR), but an intermediate region IR better preserves discovered parallelism to GPU/CGRA and is the 'optimize high, lower late, pick backend last' shape. This bears DIRECTLY on the standing non-vN constraint — direct-and-only-to-LLVM couples the whole compiler to the von-Neumann target. Architecture fork; needs an owner call.
- Is the CURRENT elaborator typing genuinely on-network (net-add-propagator/net-cell-write) at HEAD, or function-call-shaped dispatched through registries? The phase-collapse thesis and the present-tense 'the compiler IS a propagator network' depend on this; the Network Reality Check rule exists because PPN Track 4 built function-call wrappers around imperative infer. Run the code-grounding facet that was never run before treating typing-collapse as shipped.
- Does the .pnet network-as-value round-trip wait on PM-13 (handler-as-data) and NTT (propagators-as-data) — or can Q2a partition: ship the cell-table LLVM-directive declarations FIRST (they already serialize, the merge-fn aside), defer the symbol-ref merge/body table + topology round-trip second? The Q2b directive vocabulary is ready today; the Q2a closure/sentinel fix is the unbuilt linchpin.
- PReduce-first-then-LLVM (6+ months) vs the collaborator's LLVM prototype needing reduction-on-propagators NOW: ship a NAIVE on-network reducer (Track 1+2 minimal, eager-only, tree-cost) to unblock the collaborator while the saturation/extraction story matures, or accept the collaborator rebases later? The operational spur is in tension with the 'port a fast algorithm' ordering discipline.
- Has the NAC (Negative Application Condition) first-class-rule-schema requirement been owner-acknowledged before Track 0.2 opens? Without NACs, expansive rules (x→(+ x 0)) don't terminate; it's flagged as an owner-approval point in the engineering memo but no evidence of acknowledgment in the read docs.
- Does the C3.e merge target hold — ONE residual operator (\_Q : Q×M→M) for BOTH PReduce cost-extraction AND Logic-Engine backward-chaining? It's an explicit FALSIFICATION TARGET, not a confirmed result; the unification refactor hasn't been done. If a backward-chaining operation that doesn't fit the signature surfaces, the merge fails and Track 4's code-reduction claim with it.
- Is the network-as-OPTIMIZING-IR also a good network-as-EXECUTION-representation, or do you need a SECOND locality-optimized lowered form (HVM2-style flat array / columnar SoA) for both LLVM codegen AND non-vN execution, distinct from the CHAMP network used for the fixpoint? V8's 7× L1-miss retreat from sea-of-nodes says the pointer-graph is a poor execution rep — but a second form partially contradicts the 'one substrate, no separate IR' aesthetic.


## near_term_sketch

DE-RISK THE TWO HIGHEST-UNCERTAINTY CLAIMS (PReduce↔LLVM coupling + phase-collapse) WITH THREE SMALL, FALSIFYING ARTIFACTS BEFORE ANY TRACK-1 SUBSTRATE BUILD. None requires a full PReduce or LLVM port; each produces a number that resolves an open question.

ARTIFACT 1 — THE E-CLASS-SIZE-DISTRIBUTION PROBE (falsifies the saturation-default + fat-e-class assumption; ~1-2 days). Instrument the EXISTING imperative reduction.rkt (or a minimal on-network β/δ/ι prototype on a handful of acceptance files) to record, per reduction site, how many DISTINCT cost-differing equivalent forms actually arise. If — like Cranelift's 1.13 avg / ~88% singletons — Prologos is mostly singleton, this re-scopes Tracks 4/6 from central to rare-case and tells you S0 should default to EAGER cost-reducing rewriting (no held alternatives) for the deterministic rule kinds, reserving saturation for the genuinely non-confluent minority. FALSIFICATION CRITERION: if median e-class size > ~2-3 across representative reductions, the saturation machinery earns its keep; if ≈1, it does not — and you've saved Track 1 from building heavy union-find/multi-form/worldview-tagged storage the common case never uses. This is the single most decision-altering measurement in the sweep.

ARTIFACT 2 — THE TREE-vs-DAG EXTRACTION-COST MICROBENCH (falsifies 'the residual IS the extractor'; ~2-3 days). On a small hand-built e-graph with genuine sharing (a few shared subexpressions reused 3-5×), compute BOTH (a) the tropical-residual/greedy-DP tree-cost extraction (what PPN 4C Phase 1B ships) and (b) the DAG/sharing-aware optimum (tiny brute-force or a 10-line ILP). Measure the QUALITY GAP (how much worse is tree-cost when sharing is real) and confirm the residual does NOT recover the DAG optimum. This empirically settles the over-claim at E_GRAPHS §471 and gives the number that decides open-question #1: if the gap is small on Prologos-shaped terms, accept tree-cost + scope residuation to provenance; if large, commit to a DAG-cost backend (e-boost warm-start-ILP / treewidth-FPT — and measure the typical treewidth of lowered .pnet e-graphs as a sub-probe, since ≤~10 makes optimal DAG extraction poly-time and largely moots the NP-hardness for real modules).

ARTIFACT 3 — THE Q2b CELL-DECLARATION → LLVM-EMISSION SPIKE + opt-VERIFICATION GATE (falsifies the call-shim trap; de-risks the lowering; ~3-5 days). Take ONE well-known specialized cell that already ships — the fuel-cost cell (:tier 'hot, :storage 'monotone-counter, :fires-on 'threshold-crossing; propagator.rkt:1077) — and hand-write the LLVM IR it SHOULD lower to: an atomic-fixnum cell with inlined fast-path increment and dependent-notification elided except on threshold-crossing, NO opaque runtime call. Then run the VERIFICATION GATE: `opt` asserts the hot kernel is (i) free of opaque calls, (ii) carries the declared memory()/noalias attributes (LLVM's own inference confirms), (iii) survives mem2reg with zero residual entry-block allocas. This proves the specialized-cell-declaration → LLVM-directive mapping (IG4) is real AND that the call-shim trap is avoidable for at least one production cell — establishing the emission checklist (FR3) as the concrete Q2b answer rather than an aspiration. This spike is the 'Network Reality Check' analog for the LLVM boundary and is the template the eventual Track-0.3 substrate-call-boundary follows.

SEQUENCING: run Artifact 1 FIRST (it can re-scope everything downstream); Artifacts 2 and 3 in parallel after. All three are pre-Track-1, cost <2 weeks total, and each kills a specific over-claim or unblocks a specific decision — the disciplined alternative to building the substrate on assumptions the Cranelift/extraction-NP frontier has already falsified. SEPARATELY, before opening Track 0.2: get owner acknowledgment of the NAC requirement, and fix the structurally-invalid Schlatt arXiv ID '2602.16707' in E_GRAPHS_RESEARCH + PREDUCE_MASTER.

# Critique (full)


## coverage_assessment

The 12-facet union is genuinely strong and unusually honest — the internal facets (IG1-IG6) are well-grounded against HEAD e5ede4b2 (verified; I re-confirmed) and the frontier facets (FR1-FR6) bring real 2024-2026 prior art that materially corrects two over-claims in our own docs. Coverage of the human's three asks is asymmetric: Q1 (PReduce + LLVM-aware design) is covered thoroughly and the LLVM-lowering hooks are well-surfaced (IG1, IG4, FR3); Q2 (.pnet→optimal-LLVM) is covered at the vocabulary/checklist level (IG4 specialized-cell-meta→directive bridge; FR3 the canonical LLVM-frontend checklist) but the operational seam — how an extracted term hands off to Track-2 lowering, before-or-interleaved — is explicitly undefined (IG1, IG4 both flag this). The standing GPU/non-vN constraint is covered better than I expected (FR5 is the strongest single facet here, with GDlog/Gunrock/CGRA-via-eqsat as concrete targets), but every non-vN claim in the IG facets is INFERRED, not doc-grounded — the term 'non-von-Neumann' barely appears in our corpus.

THREE things I independently verified that sharpen the picture: (1) HEAD is e5ede4b2 as claimed. (2) The E_GRAPHS_RESEARCH doc internally CONTRADICTS itself on extraction: §2.5 (line 118) and §6.3 (lines 375-383) correctly state DAG/sharing-aware extraction is NP-hard, but §6.2 (line 188) and §7.5 (line 471) over-claim 'we don't need to engineer an extractor — the residual IS the extraction algorithm' and 'ILP-based extraction is residuation in a richer cost quantale where sharing is encoded structurally.' (3) The TROPICAL_QUANTALE doc itself (§3.3 line 185) admits residuation is 'a STRUCTURAL existence guarantee, not an algorithmic one' — which directly falsifies the §7.5 'inherit the algebra, no extractor needed' framing for the DAG case. FR6 and FR1 caught this correctly and it is the single highest-value correction in the whole sweep.

The biggest coverage HOLE: no facet grounds the claim that the CURRENT elaborator typing is actually on-network (net-add-propagator/net-cell-write) vs function-call-shaped at HEAD. IG3 flags this explicitly as out-of-scope and routes it to a code-grounding facet that was never run. Since phase-collapse is the load-bearing thesis and the Network Reality Check rule exists precisely because PPN Track 4 risked exactly this divergence, the sweep validated the VISION extensively without grounding whether the typing phase has already collapsed onto the network in code. That is the gap the main session must close before treating 'the compiler IS a propagator network' as present-tense rather than aspirational.


## websearch_health

REAL web search in 5 of 6 frontier facets; 1 partial. FR1, FR2, FR3, FR4, FR5, FR6 all return concrete external URLs with specific, checkable bibliographic detail (arXiv IDs, venue+year, author lists, page-level claims) — this is not memory fabrication. Spot-evidence of genuine fetching: FR1 cites Fallin's Apr-2026 Cranelift aegraph retrospective with NON-memorizable production metrics (1.13 avg e-nodes/e-class, 2-suboptimal-extractions-in-4M-value-nodes, 7-8% compile overhead) — these are not in any pre-2026 training corpus and could only come from fetching cfallin.org. FR2 cites V8's 2025 'leaving Sea of Nodes' post with specific numbers (compile time halved, 7x fewer L1 dcache misses). FR6 cites a Nov-2025 arXiv (2511.18099 Ternary Tropical Gamma Semiring) and Nov-2025 (2511.20782 Optimism in Equality Saturation, PLDI 2026) — both post-date a Jan-2026 cutoff only narrowly and are cited with paper-specific structure, indicating fetch not recall.

PARTIAL/honest-failure flags worth trusting: FR3 self-reports '10 searches + 4 successful fetches (3 fetches blocked by paywall/PDF-compression; substituted with HTML mirrors and search snippets)' — this is the signature of a real search session, not a memory dump (a memory fallback would not report fetch failures). FR4 self-reports '10 searches + 4 successful fetches (3 blocked by paywall/PDF)' and explicitly tags two claims as FROM-MEMORY/unverified (MLIR dataflow framework; an MLIR comparison) — exemplary honesty. FR1 notes one PDF (Tate eqsat) 'rendered as binary on fetch; content corroborated from POPL09/abstract.'

NET: websearch health is good and the facets are appropriately self-skeptical. The one thing to watch — FR6 and FR1 both lean on very-recent (Nov-2025/2026) papers near the knowledge cutoff; the main session should re-verify the 2026 arXiv IDs (2511.20782 optimism-in-eqsat; 2511.18099 TTGS; the Schlatt 2602.16707 'persistent compiler abstraction' ID looks malformed — 2602.* is not a valid arXiv YYMM, likely a hallucinated or transcription-garbled identifier) before citing them in any external write-up. Flag: the Schlatt arXiv ID '2602.16707' appearing in IG1/IG3/FR1 is almost certainly wrong (no 2602 month exists); verify the real ID.


## novelty_verdict

BRUTALLY HONEST: the headline 'the compiler collapses to a lattice fixpoint / the network IS a super-optimizing compiler' is ~85% well-trodden and ~15% genuinely novel, and the novel 15% is NOT where the docs put their rhetorical weight.

WELL-TRODDEN (each conjunct independently anticipated, most by decades):
- 'optimization/analysis = least fixpoint of a monotone function on a lattice': Kildall 1973, Cousot-Cousot 1977, Tarski 1955. Founding, not novel. (FR4)
- 'analysis+optimization fused in ONE lattice fixpoint, in production, provably stronger than sequential passes': SCCP (Wegman-Zadeck 1985), shipping in LLVM/GCC for 40 years. The phase-collapse precision argument Prologos wants to make IS the SCCP argument. (FR4)
- 'the COMPILER ITSELF as abstract interpretation, passes as functor domains, SSA derived as an abstract domain': Bautista-Jensen-Monat-Miné, PLDI 2024 — directly anticipates 'compiler-as-fixpoint.' (FR4)
- 'optimization is non-destructive rewriting on a shared IR + separate cost extraction' (= equality saturation, = the heart of PReduce): Tate-Stepp-Tatlock-Lerner 2009; egg 2021. (FR1, FR2)
- 'value-based IR dissolves scheduling/phase-ordering': Click sea-of-nodes ~1995 — AND V8 ABANDONED it in 2025 (FR2), which is a live counter-datapoint the docs do not metabolize.
- 'one fixpoint system unifying Datalog + equality saturation + abstract-interpretation, with optimistic (TOP-start) e-class analysis': egglog PLDI 2025 + 'Optimism in Equality Saturation' PLDI 2026. This is converging on Prologos's PReduce thesis IN REAL TIME on the SAME e-graph+Datalog+AI triad. (FR4, FR1)
- 'same DSL for mid-end rewrites AND instruction lowering, one rule registry': Cranelift ISLE, in production (FR1).
- 'eqsat-to-optimal-machine-code via extraction + constraint solver': Denali, PLDI 2002 — 24 years old (FR1).
- 'lattice-semantics language, fixpoints of monotone functions on user-declared lattices': Flix (PLDI 2016), Datafun (ICFP 2016) (FR4).
- 'tropical/semiring cost, Kleene-star = all-pairs shortest path for extraction': Goodman 1999 + the entire Floyd-Warshall-meets-Kleene literature (FR6, our own TROPICAL_QUANTALE §2.4).
- 'multi-dimensional tropical cost for joint objectives': Ternary Tropical Gamma Semiring, Nov-2025 (FR6).

WHERE THE GENUINELY NOVEL CONTRIBUTION ACTUALLY IS (the 15%, ranked by defensibility):
1. TOTALIZATION ON ONE PROPAGATOR SUBSTRATE — running the WHOLE front-to-back compiler (lex→parse→elaborate→dependent-type/QTT/session-check→reduce→codegen→logic-programming) as ONE BSP-scheduled monotone propagator network where the network IS the IR, under explicit cell/propagator/scheduler orthogonality (scheduler-portable). NO surveyed system collapses the entire compiler this way — all prior art fuses a BOUNDED set (SCCP: 2 analyses; AI-as-compiler: an analysis-derived pass pipeline; egglog: rewriting+analysis over terms; Lerner-Grove-Chambers: a fixed handful of dataflow analyses). This is the conjunction claim, and it is real — but it is a BREADTH/SUBSTRATE claim, not a 'new fixpoint idea,' and a reviewer who knows AI-as-compilation + egglog + Flix will find every per-phase conjunct familiar. FR4's verdict is exactly right: lead with breadth+substrate+scheduler-portability, NEVER with 'it's a fixpoint.'

2. SCHEDULER-PORTABILITY OF THE WHOLE COMPILER AS DEPLOYMENT THESIS — the .pnet network-as-value rehydrates and runs to the SAME fixpoint under Gauss-Seidel / BSP / Zig-LLVM / future distributed (IG2, IG5). This is the LVars/CALM/BloomL order-independence guarantee lifted from a runtime data-structure library to an entire COMPILER. Genuinely distinctive framing; the empirical witness (E9-bis: 40-line parallel scheduler 'just worked' first-try) is the strongest single piece of evidence for CALM-as-structural in the corpus.

3. TROPICAL-QUANTALE RESIDUATION FOR COST-PROVENANCE (not extraction-speed) — no facet found e-graph cost EXTRACTION framed as quantale residuation (FR6 confirms the seam is unoccupied, though adjacent to active enriched-category work). BUT — and this is the brutal part — the residuation contribution is novel ONLY for backward cost-BLAME/provenance, NOT as a faster or NP-escaping extractor (see challenges). The docs over-claim it as 'the extraction algorithm.' Scoped to provenance, it's a real, defensible, small contribution.

4. HASSE-PARALLEL-DECOMPOSITION (Hyperlattice optimality claim) — this is the WEAKEST novelty claim because (a) our own docs admit it is 'a design mandate, not a theorem' (DESIGN_PRINCIPLES.org:441), (b) it is CONFIRMED only for Boolean (Hasse=hypercube, textbook) and STRONG-but-prior-art for distributive (Garg LLP, SPAA 2020), and (c) the universal-across-varieties form is flagged APPARENT-NOVELTY-pending-falsification by the PTF doc itself. The single genuinely-novel sub-claim here is self-hosting closure (#4 in the PTF §7.3 list): the compiler that compiles Hasse-driven programs is itself a Hasse-driven network — Garg has no analog. But that is a recursion claim, not a parallelism result.

VERDICT IN ONE SENTENCE: the novelty is the CONJUNCTION at full-compiler scope on a scheduler-portable propagator substrate — not any single conjunct, not the fixpoint idea, not eqsat, not the cost algebra. Sell it as 'first totalizing, scheduler-portable, dependently-typed propagator-network compiler,' cite SCCP/AI-as-compilation/egglog/Denali AS the lineage you extend (which strengthens credibility), and DROP any framing that implies 'compiler-as-fixpoint' or 'extraction-as-residuation-escapes-NP' is itself new. Whether the conjunction is also FASTER/BETTER than the same techniques bolted together conventionally is the genuinely-open empirical question every honest facet flags (§6 speculative).


## challenges

- **claim**: Extraction = tropical-quantale residuation, inherited for free from PPN 4C Phase 1B; 'we don't need to engineer an extractor' (E_GRAPHS_RESEARCH §7.5 line 471).; **challenge**: FALSE for the cost model real codegen needs, and our OWN doc proves it. Residuation computes TREE cost (each shared subexpr counted per-use) in poly time; the DAG/sharing-aware cost LLVM actually wants (each emitted instruction counted ONCE) is NP-complete AND inapproximable to any constant factor (FR1/FR6: reduction from min-set-cover; e-graph↔monotone-Boolean-circuit bijection, arXiv 2408.17042). TROPICAL_QUANTALE §3.3 (line 185, VERIFIED) itself admits residuation is 'a STRUCTURAL existence guarantee, not an algorithmic one.' E_GRAPHS_RESEARCH §6.2 line 188's claim 'ILP-based extraction is residuation in a richer cost quantale where sharing is encoded structurally' is the precise conflation: encoding sharing structurally is exactly what makes the residual NON-product/non-local and therefore NP-hard — the algebra does not dissolve the combinatorics. Action: Track 4 MUST split tree-cost (residual, fine) from DAG-cost (needs e-boost warm-start-ILP / treewidth-FPT / sparse-extraction); the residuation contribution is genuine only for cost-PROVENANCE, not extraction. This is the single most important correction in the sweep and it requires editing two of our own docs.
- **claim**: The propagator network IS the IR; emit OPTIMAL LLVM from the lattice/cell runtime (Q2b).; **challenge**: The runtime-on-cells model is structurally PRONE to the call-shim trap that is the #1 measurable defeater of LLVM passes (FR3): if cell reads/writes and propagator firing lower to a sequence of opaque runtime-library calls, LLVM cannot inline, alias-analyze, eliminate redundant loads, SROA, or vectorize ACROSS them. CHAMP/hasheq persistent cell stores are pointer-rich — the OPPOSITE of the coalesced/SSA-promotable layout LLVM wants (and the same pattern that gave V8 7x L1 dcache misses, FR2/FR5). Avoiding the trap is POSSIBLE but is real work the docs have not scoped: (a) a flat columnar/SoA mirror of cell state for the backend; (b) pure-SSA emission for single-write propagators with entry-block allocas only; (c) custom calling convention (musttail+JWA/ghccc) for the dispatch loop; (d) confine opaque calls strictly to the effect/FFI stratum; (e) derive TBAA disjoint-alias roots from SRE domains; (f) exploit QTT m0/m1/mw for Perceus-style precise-RC to AVOID gc.statepoint barriers entirely. None of (a)-(f) is designed today. 'The network IS the IR' is true for the OPTIMIZING IR; it is a CATEGORY ERROR for the EXECUTION representation — V8's 2025 retreat from sea-of-nodes is the cautionary production datapoint.
- **claim**: Phase collapse: the multi-pass compiler collapses to one lattice fixpoint; phases become strata; cross-phase info-flow is cell-state propagation.; **challenge**: TWO challenges. (1) NOT-YET-GROUNDED: no facet confirms the CURRENT elaborator typing is actually net-add-propagator/net-cell-write at HEAD vs function-call-shaped dispatched through registries. IG3 flags exactly this and routes it to a code-grounding facet that was never run. The Network Reality Check rule (in CLAUDE.md) EXISTS because PPN Track 4 'diverged from its D.3 design: three critique rounds validated a propagator-native architecture, but implementation built function-call wrappers around the same imperative infer.' So the present-tense 'the compiler IS a propagator network' may be aspirational for the typing phase too — not just for reduction. (2) RELOCATED-NOT-ELIMINATED: the canon (FR2) shows phase collapse dissolves only the MONOTONE inter-pass ordering; the genuinely-hard residue (non-monotone/destructive choices + NP-hard cost extraction) RELOCATES to (a) the strata above S0 and (b) tropical extraction. 'Stratum assignment' is itself a phase order — defensible ONLY if you PROVE strata are ordered by a forced fixpoint-requirement partial-order derived from each rule's algebra, not chosen for performance. The docs assert this derivation; they do not exhibit it. Honest claim: 'monotone phase ordering dissolved into S0; residual hardness relocated to stratum-structure + extraction, not removed.'
- **claim**: GPU/GPGPU and non-von-Neumann targets stay in reach because the lattice fixpoint is order-independent (CALM).; **challenge**: The reach is CONCRETE in principle (FR5 found real targets: GDlog Datalog-on-GPU is almost a literal blueprint; Gunrock frontier = explicit per-round activation set; CGRA mapping via equality saturation = FlexC, a stunning PReduce synergy) — BUT every non-vN claim in the IG facets is INFERRED, the term barely appears in our corpus, and FR5 names four hard blockers the vision has not addressed: (1) the COMMON-CASE granularity mismatch — Cranelift measures avg e-class = 1.13 e-nodes and GPUs want thousands of uniform work-items, so the COMPILER'S OWN networks may never be wide enough to benefit (GPU is plausibly only for USER programs compiled to big BSP-LE/e-graph/relational fixpoints, not for the compiler itself); (2) pointer-chasing CHAMP stores are GPU-hostile and need a columnar mirror that does not exist; (3) heterogeneous propagators-per-round cause warp divergence unless bucketed by propagator-kind; (4) dynamic topology (cells allocated mid-fixpoint) is hostile to any static fabric and is tolerable only if bounded to stratum boundaries with EBM-style pre-reservation. The honest status: the ARCHITECTURE keeps non-vN reachable (orthogonality + ACI merges + explicit activation sets are the cheap-to-lock-in invariants), but no measurement shows the compiler's networks are wide enough to WANT it, and the columnar/activation-set/propagator-bucketing engineering is unscoped.
- **claim**: Cranelift/eqsat/Schlatt validate that 'the e-graph IS the IR' and PReduce just inherits the win.; **challenge**: The single most important PRODUCTION datapoint (FR1, Fallin Apr-2026, VERIFIED non-memorizable metrics) CONTRADICTS PReduce's saturate-to-fixpoint default and should re-scope Tracks 1/4/6 BEFORE the substrate is built: Cranelift's aegraph is EAGER single-pass cost-reducing rewriting (NOT saturation), avg e-class = 1.13 e-nodes (~88% singletons), only 2 suboptimal extractions in 4 MILLION value nodes, and the EXPENSIVE part of a true e-graph (saturation + fat-e-class extraction) bought LITTLE — most of the ~2% runtime win came from structural-sharing + good code-placement (the cheap part), at 7-8% MORE compile time. Implication: PReduce's S0 'monotone rewriting stratum' implicitly assumes saturation for everything — the regime Cranelift abandoned. PReduce needs an EAGER-vs-SATURATE axis per rule-kind (IN-fragment/confluence-by-construction/arithmetic run eager; reserve saturation for genuinely non-confluent cost-differing rules), and MUST measure the e-class-size distribution on real Prologos reduction BEFORE Track 1 builds the heavy union-find/multi-form/worldview-tagged machinery. If Prologos is also ~88% singletons, Tracks 4/6 drop from 'central' to 'rare-case.'
- **claim**: The novelty is genuine because each piece composes on one substrate (PReduce/SH thesis).; **challenge**: The convergent prior art is arriving FASTER than the implementation. egglog (PLDI 2025) + 'Optimism in Equality Saturation' (PLDI 2026) already unify Datalog + equality saturation + abstract interpretation into ONE lattice fixpoint with optimistic (SCCP-style TOP-start) e-class analysis + non-destructive rewriting — i.e. the analysis+rewriting HALF of PReduce, on the SAME e-graph+Datalog+AI triad, peer-reviewed and shipping, while PReduce Track 1 has zero code (status ⬜). The differentiators that survive (propagator substrate + scheduler-portability + dependent-type/QTT awareness + same-substrate-also-hosts-parsing/typing + cross-SESSION .pnet persistence) are REAL but NARROW, and two of them (cross-session persistence; the totalizing substrate) are the ONLY ones with no published peer. The risk: by the time PReduce lands (gated behind PReduce-first-then-LLVM, ~6+ months per IG2), more of the conjunction may be independently published. Action: the defensible novelty must be staked on cross-session persistence + totalization NOW, and the design must explicitly position against egglog/optimism-in-eqsat as the nearest peers rather than generic 'egg.'
- **claim**: .pnet round-trips the network so it lowers to LLVM (the deployment-artifact / network-as-value linchpin).; **challenge**: The linchpin is UNBUILT and the closure problem is the blocker, per our own docs (IG4, VERIFIED pnet-serialize.rkt:99,104,440): today .pnet REPLACES the live prop-network with a '(runtime-prop-network) sentinel and rebuilds FRESH on load — topology is LOST, not serialized; merge-functions/fire-functions are Racket CLOSURES that become 'foreign-proc stubs. So 'the .pnet IS the IR' is true ONLY for elaboration-state values + 17 registries, NOT for the live network that did the elaborating. The named fix (merge-fns → symbolic refs in the SRE form registry, propagators-as-DATA, NTT-style) is 'partly upstream of NTT progress' and undesigned. Crucial nuance IG4 surfaces: the Q2b LLVM-DIRECTIVE vocabulary (specialized-cell-meta tier/storage/fires-on) is ALREADY plain-symbol struct fields exercised in 8 production cells, so it serializes trivially TODAY — but the Q2a topology+merge round-trip (the part that makes .pnet a genuine network-as-value) is the hard, unbuilt half. The two should be partitioned: cell-table declarations first (unblocks Q2b lowering experiments), symbol-ref merge/body table second (unblocks Q2a). Treating 'the .pnet is the IR' as present-tense is wrong; it is a target.


## tension_points

- ORDERING: PReduce-first-then-LLVM (IG2/IG6 explicit: 'porting before PReduce = porting a slow algorithm to a slightly-less-slow algorithm') vs the collaborator's LLVM prototype needing reduction-on-propagators NOW. Resolution forces a sequencing call: ship a NAIVE on-network reducer (Track 1+2 minimal, eager-only, tree-cost) to unblock the collaborator while the full saturation/extraction story matures — or accept the collaborator rebases later. The 6+-month PReduce gate (IG2) is in tension with the operational spur (IG1 Origin).
- TREE-COST vs DAG-COST is the pivotal Track-4 decision that determines whether the super-optimization claim is real or over-stated: tree-cost (residual, poly-time, but double-counts sharing → suboptimal LLVM) vs DAG-cost (faithful to emitted-instruction sharing, NP-hard → needs ILP/treewidth/sparse machinery the design does not plan). You cannot have both 'inherit the residual for free' AND 'optimal LLVM.' Pick one and edit E_GRAPHS_RESEARCH §7.5 + TROPICAL_QUANTALE §10.7 accordingly.
- EAGER vs SATURATE per rule-kind (forced by Cranelift's 1.13-e-nodes production finding): the whole S0 'monotone rewriting stratum' design assumes saturation; the one hard production datapoint chose eager-cost-reducing single-pass and found fat e-classes don't pay. Resolving this requires a MEASUREMENT (e-class-size distribution on real Prologos reduction) BEFORE Track 1 commits the heavy substrate — and the measurement could re-scope Tracks 4/6 from central to rare-case.
- WHERE sharing/register-pressure/code-motion cost LIVES: in PReduce's e-graph extraction (DAG-cost, NP-complete, makes Track 4 hard) vs deferred to an LLVM-side placement pass (Cranelift's choice, keeps PReduce extraction tree-cheap, FR1/FR3 both recommend this). 'Collapse the front, phase-separate the back' is the production-validated topology — but it means PReduce does NOT do the sharing-aware optimization, which dents the 'super-optimizing' claim's scope.
- THE NETWORK-AS-OPTIMIZING-IR vs NETWORK-AS-EXECUTION-REPRESENTATION split: the pointer-graph propagator network is great for expressing the monotone fixpoint and terrible for cache-local execution (V8 datapoint). Resolution: a SECOND locality-optimized lowered form (HVM2-style flat array / columnar SoA) for both LLVM codegen AND non-vN execution, distinct from the network used for the fixpoint. This is unscoped and partially contradicts the 'one substrate, no separate IR' aesthetic.
- DIRECT-to-LLVM vs through-an-intermediate-region-IR (RVSDG-like / eqsat-MLIR dialect): the docs commit to .pnet-DIRECT-to-LLVM (no MLIR, to preserve the single-fixpoint property, IG1/concurrency-primitives §1) — but FR3/FR5 show an intermediate regionalized-dataflow IR better preserves the discovered parallelism (the 'all-in-parallel' mantra) all the way to a GPU/CGRA backend, and is the production-validated 'optimize high, lower late, pick backend last' shape. Direct-to-LLVM couples the whole compiler to the von-Neumann target; an intermediate region IR keeps GPU/dataflow siblings reachable. This is a genuine architecture fork the human must decide, and it bears directly on the standing non-vN constraint.
- PHASE COLLAPSE SCOPE: collapse typing/elaboration/reduction (PPN 4D ambition) BUT keep a phase boundary before codegen extraction+lowering (Cranelift: lowering needs a SETTLED cost surface; you can't pick instructions while the e-graph still grows). 'Collapse the front, phase-separate the back' contradicts the maximalist 'codegen rules fire on the same network as propagators' framing (super-optimizing-compiler §3.1, which FR1 flags as the least-grounded leg with no concrete codegen-propagator design).
- VALIDATED-≠-DEPLOYED on the thesis itself: the corpus has DONE phase-collapse across several phase-pairs (E1: type-inference↔constraint-resolution dissolved within S0) but had not NAMED it as one isolated claim until the utm-fl audit, and reduction is explicitly NOT yet collapsed (reduction.rkt imperative). The FULL claim is aspirational-pending-PReduce; the PARTIAL claim (everything-except-evaluation) is rigorous. The human must decide whether A0/the external story waits for PReduce (chosen, IG2) or scope-limits the claim honestly — and must ground whether even the typing collapse is genuinely net-add-propagator at HEAD (the un-run code-grounding gap).
